**[00:32:00.590] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000037)

**[00:32:08.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000038)

**[00:32:27.389] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000041)

**[00:32:37.470] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000042)

**[00:32:43.419] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000043)

**[00:32:47.029] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000044)

**[00:32:52.909] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000045)

**[00:32:55.789] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000046)

**[00:36:21.766] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000120)

**[00:36:28.680] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000121)

**[00:36:50.040] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000123)

**[00:37:40.662] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000128)

**[00:37:54.265] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000130)

**[00:37:56.924] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000131)  
In the case of the BIR's charter, five percent of the agencies' income from gaming operations

**[00:37:59.310] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000132)  
goes to the BIR as franchise tax. The 50 percent of the 95 percent balance of gaming revenues

**[00:38:03.900] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000133)  
goes to the National Treasury as national government's mandated income share. Half

**[00:38:10.390] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000134)  
of the said governments' share remitted to the National Treasury is allocated or goes

**[00:38:13.970] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000135)  
PAGCOR's total contributions to nation-building reached P66.95 billion in 2025,

**[00:38:20.750] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000136)  
are given fixed amount for their respective community development projects.

**[00:38:25.990] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000137)  
PAGCOR also allocates part of its revenues for socio-civic projects.

**[00:38:32.294] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000138)  
Pursuant to the National Internal Revenue Code,

**[00:38:35.150] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000139)  
PAGCOR is also paying corporate income tax to the BIR.

**[00:38:40.950] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000140)  
Special laws also top PAGCOR as a major funding source for the Philippine Sports Commission,

**[00:38:46.390] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000141)  
such as the 5% PSC share and the sports benefit and incentives for athletes and coaches.

**[00:38:51.790] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000142)

**[00:39:02.720] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000143)  
We now go to PAGCOR's significant accomplishments in 2025 and the first semester of 2026.

**[00:39:12.150] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000144)  
In 2025, the agency posted P95.14 billion in income from gaming operations,

**[00:39:15.069] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000145)  
lower by P2.38 billion,

**[00:39:19.069] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000146)  
or around 2.5% compared to the P97.52 billion in 2024.

**[00:39:25.870] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000147)  
Among the factors that declined in the gaming revenues in 2025 compared to the previous year

**[00:39:30.750] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000148)  
was the closure of Pogo in the middle of December 2024,

**[00:39:34.109] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000149)  
decline in gaming revenues of PAGCOR casinos,

**[00:39:36.910] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000150)  
as well as decline in gaming revenues of our licenses.

**[00:39:43.030] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000151)  
While the delinking of e-wallets from online gaming platforms

**[00:39:45.829] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000152)  
in the third quarter of the year 2020,

**[00:39:48.910] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000153)  
also slowed down the income.

**[00:39:58.870] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000154)  
The agency posted P106.03 billion in total income in 2025,

**[00:40:05.894] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000155)  
lower by P5.68 billion compared to the P111.71 billion total income in 2024.

**[00:40:17.190] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000156)  
PAGCOR's total contribution to nation-building reached P66.95 billion in 2025,

**[00:40:25.040] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000157)  
lower by P1.25 billion compared to the P68.20 billion contribution in 2024.

**[00:40:30.790] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000159)  
This is due to the lower revenues in 2025.

**[00:40:38.680] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000160)  
Also in 2025, PAGCOR remitted a total of P8.31 billion in taxes to the Bureau of Internal Revenue.

**[00:40:45.150] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000161)  
Of this amount, P4.78 billion represents PAGCOR franchise tax,

**[00:40:49.040] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000162)  
P2.63 billion within withholding taxes,

**[00:40:52.280] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000163)  
and P0.9 billion in corporate income tax.

**[00:41:00.625] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000164)  
On top of its contribution to nation-building,

**[00:41:04.275] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000165)  
PAGCOR declares and remits at least 50% of its annual net earnings as cash dividends

**[00:41:07.990] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000166)  
to the national government.

**[00:41:10.230] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000167)  
This is pursuant to Republic Act No. 7656 or the Dividends Law.

**[00:41:14.311] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000168)  
For 2025, PAGCOR declared P5.67 billion in dividends.

**[00:41:25.230] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000169)  
Based on the guidelines on the operationalization,

**[00:41:27.589] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000170)  
of the allocations appropriations for RA11223 or the Universal Healthcare Act of 2019,

**[00:41:37.550] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000171)  
PAGCOR shall remit 50% of its gaming revenue to the Bureau of Treasury every 20th of the month

**[00:41:44.510] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000172)  
and 50% of it will be allocated for the UHC.

**[00:41:49.520] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000173)  
The allocated funds shall be used exclusively for the improvement of benefits packages

**[00:41:53.790] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000174)  
under the PhilHealth's National Health Insurance Program.

**[00:41:58.990] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000175)  
And PAGCOR shall be consulted in the preparation of its Medium-Term Expenditure Program Plan.

**[00:42:08.720] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000176)  
For 2025, the 50% of the 50% government share for allocation to the UHC is around P22.56 billion.

**[00:42:22.950] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000177)

**[00:42:31.316] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Maria Cheryl I. De Guia** (asr-000179)

**[00:43:13.230] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000189)  
On March 25, 2026, PAGCOR, in partnership with the Department of Education,

**[00:43:19.589] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000190)  
launched a nationwide school building program to help address the country's classroom shortage

**[00:43:24.630] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000191)  
under the instructions of President Ferdinand R. Marcos, Jr.

**[00:43:28.140] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000192)  
Under the program,

**[00:43:31.031] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000193)  
PAGCOR has committed the amount of 7.5 billion pesos

**[00:43:34.510] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000194)  
to fund the construction of 125 three-story 12-classroom school buildings

**[00:43:40.990] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000195)  
equivalent to approximately 1,500 classrooms over the next two years.

**[00:43:49.190] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000196)  
Every facility will be equipped with student chairs and tables,

**[00:43:56.109] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000197)  
PWD-friendly amenities, electric fans, microphones, a 75-inch smart TV,

**[00:43:59.829] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000198)  
and a one-year internet subscription.

**[00:44:03.839] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000199)  
Site selection is determined upon the recommendation of the Department of Education

**[00:44:08.710] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000200)  
and the capability of recipient local government units

**[00:44:12.960] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000201)  
to complete the school buildings awarded to them.

**[00:44:14.920] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000202)  
Next slide, please.

**[00:44:20.990] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000203)

**[00:44:28.576] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000205)  
PAGCOR has been donating

**[00:44:30.685] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000206)  
has been donating

**[00:44:32.270] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000207)  
patient transport vehicles to local government units,

**[00:44:34.309] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000208)  
public hospitals, frontline agencies,

**[00:44:37.630] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000209)  
as well as non-government organizations.

**[00:44:40.160] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000210)  
Each vehicle is equipped with GPS,

**[00:44:43.510] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000211)  
an ambulance stretcher, oxygen tank, wheelchair,

**[00:44:48.829] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000212)  
and first aid kit to provide immediate medical care to those in need.

**[00:44:54.549] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000213)  
To date, PAGCOR has donated close to 250 PTVs

**[00:44:57.670] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000214)  
to various recipients across the country.

**[00:44:59.470] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000215)  
the country next slide every year destructive typhoons and other natural disasters impact

**[00:45:07.470] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000216)  
the philippines resulting in casualties and causing millions of pesos worth of losses in

**[00:45:19.589] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000217)  
agriculture, infrastructure, and livelihood. PAGCOR helps ease the effects of natural calamities by

**[00:45:25.950] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000218)  
providing immediate assistance to affected individuals and families in 2025 and in the

**[00:45:32.630] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000219)  
first semester of 2026 the agency spent a total of 300 million pesos for the purchase of food and

**[00:45:38.349] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000220)  
non-food packs for distribution in areas hit by typhoons floods earthquakes volcanic eruptions

**[00:45:43.240] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000221)  
and fires. On July 24, 2026, PAGCOR, together with the Bloomberry Cultural Foundation, Inc.,

**[00:45:58.520] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000222)  
the clark development corporation and the department of health

**[00:46:04.520] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000223)  
inaugurated the Jose B. Lingad Children's Medical Center,

**[00:46:08.430] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000224)  
the first facility completed under the clark multi-specialty medical center project

**[00:46:13.630] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000225)  
The inauguration was graced by His Excellency President Ferdinand R. Marcos Jr.

**[00:46:20.950] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000226)  
the state-of-the-art pediatric facility is equipped with advanced medical machines

**[00:46:25.549] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000227)  
including ai assisted diagnostic equipment enabling faster and more accurate diagnosis

**[00:46:33.215] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000228)  
of pediatric conditions that will significantly enhance health care delivery for children

**[00:46:37.750] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000229)  
in Central Luzon and neighboring regions. PAGCOR contributed P360 million worth of medical

**[00:46:45.549] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000230)  
equipment for the project while the bloomberry cultural foundation incorporated provided 1 billion

**[00:46:52.559] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000231)  
pesos for the construction and development of the facility. That ends our report po. We wish

**[00:47:00.950] PAGCOR Assistant Vice President Eric I. Balcos** (asr-000232)  
to thank the House Committee on Appropriations for inviting PAGCOR today.

**[01:02:02.039] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000360)  
Sana po nagagamit po talaga nila para tulungan po yung ating mga atleta.

**[01:02:06.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000361)  
Thank you, Chair Al.

**[01:02:07.799] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000362)  
But I also would like to highlight, under contributions to nation building,

**[01:02:11.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000363)  
despite the lower income, tumaas pa po yung socio-civic projects ninyo.

**[01:02:17.240] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000364)  
From P12.4 billion in 2024 to P12.8 billion in 2025,

**[01:02:27.079] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000365)  
And to 13.99 billion in 2026.

**[01:02:31.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000366)  
So we'd like to commend you on that as well po, that despite the downturn in the income,

**[01:02:36.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000367)  
you still retain the commitments around socio-civic projects.

**[01:02:41.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000368)  
Madam Chair, here, congress, dito po.

**[01:02:45.039] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000369)  
Yung clarificatory question sa talong mo kanina with regard to the payment sa Philippine Sports Commission,

**[01:02:51.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000370)  
di ba, every year nagbabayad na sila.

**[01:02:53.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000371)  
So, ibig sabihin, matodoble ang...

**[01:02:57.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000372)  
Kasi nagbayad na sila, contribution nila since 1993 sa National Treasury natin,

**[01:03:03.799] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000373)  
at the same time, ngayon magbibigyan na ulit,

**[01:03:06.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000374)  
kasama na yung binayad nila sa National Treasury.

**[01:03:09.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000375)  
So, ibig sabihin, doble-doble ang PSC ngayon, makukuha nila.

**[01:03:14.559] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000376)  
Congress, thank you very much.

**[01:03:16.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000377)  
Chair Al, may please respond to the query of representative...

**[01:03:18.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000378)  
With your permission, Chairperson Suansing, I'd like to answer Congressman Presley.

**[01:03:24.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000379)  
Go ahead.

**[01:03:26.642] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000380)  
Congressman Presley, hindi lang po doble sapagkat, tulad po ng nabanggit ko kanina, Your Honor,

**[01:03:36.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000381)  
yung dati pong contribution nung panahon na inabutan ko na,

**[01:03:40.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000382)  
nag-a-average po ng mga 180 to 190 million per month.

**[01:03:49.400] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000383)  
With the Supreme Court ruling po, na five percent po ang mandato ng PAGCOR

**[01:03:56.119] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000384)  
sa gross revenue, sa ngayon po, ay mahigit doble na po effective nung January 1,

**[01:04:03.839] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000385)  
yung aming kinocontribute.

**[01:04:06.160] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000386)  
Sa ngayon po, eh, kung di ako nagkakamalik,

**[01:04:09.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000387)  
umaabot po ng kulang 400 million na isang buwan.

**[01:04:12.839] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000388)  
Pero, over and above that, Your Honor,

**[01:04:16.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000389)  
pinagbabayad po kami ng Korte Suprema,

**[01:04:19.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000390)  
retroactive 1993.

**[01:04:24.400] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000391)  
Yun po, ay...

**[01:04:26.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000392)  
mag-a-average na naman ng kulang tatloon,

**[01:04:28.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000393)  
mahigit kumulang tatloon daang milyong piso po yung kada buwan.

**[01:04:33.240] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000394)  
Kaya po, kung ito ay talagang may implement,

**[01:04:37.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000395)  
ang magiging contribution po ng PAGCOR sa Philippine Sports Commission,

**[01:04:42.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000396)  
ay aabutin po ng kulang 700 million piso bawat buwan.

**[01:04:48.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000397)  
Ito po yung 5% na ngayon utos ng Korte Suprema sa gross revenue na magmumula,

**[01:04:56.480] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000398)  
plus, yung binigyan po kami ng sampun taon lamang ng Korte Suprema

**[01:05:02.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000399)  
na bayaran yung pagkakautang ng PAGCOR magsimula po noong 1993.

**[01:05:09.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000400)  
Madam Chair, I think we need to look into it, no?

**[01:05:13.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000401)  
Kasi, doble-doble na budget ng PSA ngayon.

**[01:05:15.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000402)  
With the payment for 3.3 billion every year for the next 10 years, ma'am,

**[01:05:20.200] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000403)  
that's sobra naman yan.

**[01:05:22.160] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000404)  
So, imagine pag-court, karamihan talaga more on...

**[01:05:25.640] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000405)  
namimigay sila, pero sobra naman kung ibibigay sa PSC yan.

**[01:05:29.240] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000406)  
I think we have to look into it, Madam Chair.

**[01:05:31.680] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000407)  
That's it. Thank you very much.

**[01:05:32.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000408)  
Thank you very much, Kong Presley.

**[01:05:33.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000409)  
Actually, I'm taking a look at the level,

**[01:05:37.480] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000410)  
the fiscal year 2027 NEP level for the PSC.

**[01:05:42.200] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000411)  
Medyo bumaba po siya in comparison to the 2026 GAA level,

**[01:05:47.319] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000412)  
pero yung binaba po is not commensurate yet to the increase in the remittances from PAGCOR.

**[01:05:55.359] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000413)  
So, we'll definitely scrutinize that on the part of the Committee on Appropriations

**[01:06:00.839] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000414)  
that whichever level we settle at Kong Presley for the final HGAB level of PSC,

**[01:06:10.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000415)  
talaga pong yung kailangan, whichever is sufficient based on their utilization

**[01:06:16.359] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000416)  
and based on the programs and projects that they have lined up for fiscal year 2027.

**[01:06:21.880] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-000417)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:06:22.119] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000418)  
Thank you po for pointing that out.

**[01:06:24.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000419)  
Chairperson, may I just make a...

**[01:06:26.284] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000420)  
May I make a clarification?

**[01:06:27.039] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000421)  
Yes, po. Chairperson, thank you.

**[01:06:28.119] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000422)  
Yung pong 37 billion, I'll be very candid here.

**[01:06:33.039] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000423)  
Hindi pa po namin naumpisahang bayaran.

**[01:06:36.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000424)  
Yung pong sinasabi kong inumpisahan namin noong January 1, 2026,

**[01:06:42.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000425)  
ay yung 5% of the gross revenue.

**[01:06:46.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000426)  
Yung pong, in fairness, yung pong halos 300 million naman,

**[01:06:51.279] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000427)  
yung po ang pinag-uusapan namin pang kasalukuyan ni PSC,

**[01:06:56.119] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000428)  
si Chairman Pato Gregorio, kung papano po.

**[01:06:59.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000429)  
And I think we are close to coming up with an agreement very soon

**[01:07:06.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000430)  
para po nang sa ganun ay magamit na niya sa kanyang mga programa

**[01:07:12.839] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000431)  
yung nasabing pagkakautang ng PAGCOR na na-rule ng Supreme Court with finality.

**[01:07:20.319] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000432)  
Thank you, Chairman Tengco.

**[01:07:21.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000433)  
So if we may request po from the PAGCOR officials,

**[01:07:25.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000434)  
copy of your remittances to the PSC if, kung pwede po,

**[01:07:31.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000435)  
January, the whole of 2025, as well as 2026 from the start of the year to this year.

**[01:07:39.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000436)  
So we can take that into account as we look through the budget of the Philippine Sports Commission.

**[01:07:45.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000437)  
Thank you very much po.

**[01:07:47.839] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000438)  
Those are my main clarificatory questions po.

**[01:07:52.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000439)  
We'll now open the floor to our distinguished colleagues.

**[01:07:55.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000440)  
To post questions and comments to the PAGCOR family,

**[01:08:00.960] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000441)  
may I respectfully remind our distinguished colleagues

**[01:08:03.799] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000442)  
that each member is given five minutes for questioning or interpolation,

**[01:08:08.119] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000443)  
excluding the response of the resource persons.

**[01:08:10.799] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000444)  
And when you hear the bell sound, it means that you have 30 seconds to wind up your questions.

**[01:08:16.159] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000445)  
As part of the longstanding and respected tradition of the House of Representatives

**[01:08:20.719] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000446)  
to accord preferential treatment to the minority members of the House,

**[01:08:24.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000447)  
followed by independence,

**[01:08:25.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000448)  
and majority members in an alternating manner,

**[01:08:29.279] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000449)  
that is how I will recognize our colleagues who have signed up for interpolations.

**[01:08:34.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000450)  
We'll start with the minority, then independent, then majority.

**[01:08:39.359] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000451)  
And with that, we'll first start with the first member of the minority,

**[01:08:44.199] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000452)  
Representative Elijah San Fernando.

**[01:08:46.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000453)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[01:08:48.759] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000454)  
Chair, thank ko sa presentation niyo po binanggit na 95 billion ng kita ng PAGCOR

**[01:08:54.679] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000455)  
ng galing sa gaming.

**[01:08:55.799] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000456)  
At sa gaming operation, ilang percent po ito ng total income ng PAGCOR?

**[01:09:00.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000457)  
Iyan po ay halos, imingit ko mo lang, 95, 96 percent din po ng total income natin.

**[01:09:08.440] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000458)  
Meron po ba tayong ballpark figure, ilan yung customers na pumapasok dito sa gaming operations,

**[01:09:15.239] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000459)  
online gaming operations?

**[01:09:16.480] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000460)  
Sa online po, ang nakarehistro po na mga customers ay meron pong 30,

**[01:09:26.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000461)  
30 million registered po yun.

**[01:09:31.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000462)  
Yung pong aktibong naglalaro, mga 10 million po.

**[01:09:36.400] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000463)  
Yung po yung active mismong naglalaro.

**[01:09:39.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000464)  
Pero ang nakarehistro po sa pangkasalukuyan, imingit ko mo lang po mga 30 million po.

**[01:09:45.759] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000465)  
Thank you, Chair, thank ko.

**[01:09:48.359] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000466)  
Nabanggit niyo po kanina, out of this 30 million registered and 10 million active players in online gaming,

**[01:09:55.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000467)  
most of them ay nanggagaling sa CDE market. Tama po ba ito, Chair?

**[01:10:00.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000468)  
Nandyan po yung malaking porsyento po, Congress.

**[01:10:06.560] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000469)  
Thank you.

**[01:10:06.760] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000470)  
Your Honor.

**[01:10:07.520] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000471)  
So kapag pinag-usapan po natin itong market ng online gaming operation natin,

**[01:10:11.640] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000472)  
pinatutungkulan po natin dito mga manggagawa, particular, mga minimum wage earner, and low income earners po.

**[01:10:18.480] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000473)  
Tama po ba?

**[01:10:19.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000474)  
Tama po, Your Honor.

**[01:10:21.320] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000475)  
Thank you.

**[01:10:22.400] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000476)  
Noong July 2025 po, may pinirmahan na memorandum.

**[01:10:25.230] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000477)  
Noong July 2025 po, may pinirmahan na memorandum of understanding ang PAGCOR at ang Adstandard Council.

**[01:10:29.600] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000478)  
Sabi po dito, ang gambling ay pasok na doon sa must-screen category.

**[01:10:34.120] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000479)  
Pwede niyo po bang ipaliwanag sa amin ano yung sakop ng must-screen category ng gambling?

**[01:10:40.000] Rep. Elijah Rumbaoa San Fernando** (asr-000480)  
Ano yung mga sakop po nito?

**[01:10:42.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000481)  
Your Honor, noong 2025, ang PAGCOR po, isa sa mga programa niya sa Responsible Gaming,

**[01:10:52.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000482)  
ang pakikipag-ugnayan po sa...

**[01:10:57.320] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000483)  
sa Adstandard Council.

**[01:10:58.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000484)  
Kung napansin ninyo, at kung naalala ko po, hearing po rito rin ng budget,

**[01:11:06.480] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000485)  
hindi pa po namin napipirmahan yung aming pakikipag-ugnayan sa Adstandard Council

**[01:11:14.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000486)  
noong huli pong hearing natin ng budget ng PAGCOR for year 2026.

**[01:11:20.719] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000487)  
Ang naganap po, at noon po naging isyong malaki,

**[01:11:24.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000488)  
yung pong lahat ng...

**[01:11:25.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000489)  
yung billboard halos sa buong Maynila at gayon din sa mga major tollways

**[01:11:32.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000490)  
ay punong... nakita po ng nating lahat ay napakadami po sa mga billboard na ito

**[01:11:40.360] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000491)  
ay nauukol sa online gaming.

**[01:11:44.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000492)  
Kaya, ako po ay naalarma rin at sa pakikipag-ugnayan po natin sa Adstandard Council

**[01:11:53.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000493)  
nagkaroon po na ngayon ng panuntunan.

**[01:11:55.679] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000494)  
O nagkaroon ngayon ng struktura.

**[01:11:58.480] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000495)  
Kung saan, una, hindi na po pwedeng mag-advertise ng lantaran

**[01:12:06.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000496)  
ang anumang uri ng online gaming operator, sino man ito,

**[01:12:13.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000497)  
gayon din naman po maski yung land-based casinos natin.

**[01:12:16.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000498)  
Kaya, sometime po, binigil ako po ng...

**[01:12:20.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000499)  
Kung di ako nagkakamali,

**[01:12:24.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000500)  
60 araw na palugit.

**[01:12:25.639] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000501)  
Ang lahat ng mga billboard na ito,

**[01:12:27.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000502)  
pero effective po noong September 2025,

**[01:12:32.320] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000503)  
naibaba na po natin lahat ito.

**[01:12:35.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000504)  
Pangalawa, hindi na po pwedeng mag-advertise na lamang

**[01:12:40.400] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000505)  
ang isang land-based operator,

**[01:12:43.280] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000506)  
gayon din po, your honor,

**[01:12:45.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000507)  
ang mga online gaming operators,

**[01:12:48.120] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000508)  
kung hindi dumadaan sa Adstandard Council.

**[01:12:54.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000509)  
Pangatlo po,

**[01:12:56.360] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000510)  
ipinagbawal na rin po ng Adstandard Council

**[01:13:00.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000511)  
ang pag-a-advertise ng online gaming or land-based casino operations

**[01:13:05.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000512)  
during prime time.

**[01:13:07.679] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000513)  
Hindi na po sila pwedeng mag-advertise between 5 to 8 o'clock in the evening.

**[01:13:16.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000514)  
At ngayon po,

**[01:13:17.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000515)  
hindi na po maaari ka maglagay ng mga advertisement

**[01:13:24.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000516)  
na nagiging...

**[01:13:28.400] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000517)  
nakalalamang po ngayon

**[01:13:29.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000518)  
sa mga advertisements na nakikita natin sa kapeligiran

**[01:13:33.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000519)  
ay meron pong paalala tungkol sa responsible gaming.

**[01:13:39.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000520)  
So, yun po ang pinakabalangkas

**[01:13:43.560] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000521)  
ng pakikipagkasunduan po

para ang pagsusugal ay hindi tama, lalong-lalong na sa kabataan

**[01:40:13.040] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000837)  
para ma-prevent natin sila.

**[01:40:14.800] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000838)  
Prevention ang kailangan natin.

**[01:40:16.800] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000839)  
Kasi hindi lang namang po matatanda o may edad

**[01:40:21.400] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000840)  
ang mga nagsusugal, kundi kabataan.

**[01:40:23.599] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000841)  
Maraming kabataan na nagsusugal ngayon.

**[01:40:26.719] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000842)  
Kinakikita nyo sa iba-iba, lalo sa Metro Manila.

**[01:40:29.599] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000843)  
Bakit?

**[01:40:30.280] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000844)  
Kasi nakikita ko po rito,

**[01:40:32.639] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000845)  
itong information dissemination.

**[01:40:33.679] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000846)  
Napakaganda po ng programa natin.

**[01:40:36.040] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000847)  
Meron, namimigay tayo ng transport vehicle sa mga pasyente,

**[01:40:42.360] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000848)  
mga food packs during a calamity.

**[01:40:45.159] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000849)  
Bakit instead of school building,

**[01:40:47.560] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000850)  
ang gawin na lang natin ay evacuation center na lang.

**[01:40:51.520] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000851)  
At idagdag natin ang information dissemination campaign

**[01:40:55.480] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000852)  
sa mga eskwilahan, magdagdag tayo ng pundo.

**[01:40:58.199] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000853)  
It's my suggestion, Mr. Chair.

**[01:41:01.679] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000854)  
Your Honor, we will take note of your suggestions.

**[01:41:06.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000855)  
Yes, we do not have, but maybe,

**[01:41:10.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000856)  
gusto ko lang pong banggitin dito.

**[01:41:13.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000857)  
Isa po talaga sa programa ng PAGCOR,

**[01:41:16.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000858)  
yung pagpapatuloy na magpagawa ng mga evacuation centers.

**[01:41:19.639] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000859)  
Pero it has been overcome by the memorandum of agreement

**[01:41:23.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000860)  
that we have signed with the Department of Education.

**[01:41:27.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000861)  
Dahil, tunay po nana,

**[01:41:29.320] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000862)  
Napakalaki din ang backlog ng mga classrooms natin, Your Honor.

**[01:41:33.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000863)  
Kaya minarapat na rin po ng Administrasyon pangasulukuyan na talagang ito o not,

**[01:41:41.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000864)  
bigyan na ng pansin yung pagpapagawa ng mga eskwelahan.

**[01:41:44.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000865)  
Pero we will consider your suggestions and, Your Honor, we will see how we can balance your suggestion with our current programs.

**[01:41:58.199] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000866)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Lastly, what is the status of PAGCOR Casino Privatization Initiatives?

**[01:42:05.920] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000867)  
Paano natin itong magiging efektibo sa ating revenue at sa pagkakawala ng maraming trabano?

**[01:42:14.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000868)  
Salamat po sa katanungan niyo, Congressman Odi.

**[01:42:19.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000869)  
Sa pangasulukuyan po ay iniintay na lang po ng PAGCOR ang desisyon ng GCG.

**[01:42:27.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000870)  
Or yung Governance Commission for GOCCs na isumiti na po namin ang aming mga datos na hiningi nila.

**[01:42:38.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000871)  
At sila po bilang nasa sa ilalim kami ang siyang magre-rekomenda sa tanggapan ng Pangulo

**[01:42:46.360] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000872)  
yung pagpapatuloy po ng tinatawag na privatization or decoupling.

**[01:42:51.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000873)  
Ang balita ko po ay baka po hindi marating ang last quarter.

**[01:42:58.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000874)  
Magkaroon na po ng desisyon tungkol dito.

**[01:43:02.119] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000875)  
Tunay po kasing napakabigat na rin ang kargan ng Casino Filipino, Your Honor.

**[01:43:08.400] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000876)  
Dahil sa panahon pong ako ay nanunungkulan sa PAGCOR,

**[01:43:14.360] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000877)  
napanglaki na po ng lugi ng Casino Filipino branches po namin.

**[01:43:21.400] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000878)  
Kaya wala na po talagang dapat gawin kung hindi.

**[01:43:25.719] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000879)  
I-unload na po ito sa pribadong sektor.

**[01:43:28.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000880)  
So, we are just waiting for the recommendation of GCG to the Office of the President.

**[01:43:36.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000881)  
And as I said, we were told it will be coming out very soon.

**[01:43:42.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000882)  
Tungkol naman po sa maapekto ang mga empleyado,

**[01:43:46.280] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000883)  
ako po ay imikot na halos sa lahat ng ating mga Casino Filipino sites sa buong Pilipinas.

**[01:43:53.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000884)  
At pinaliwanag ko po sa lahat ng empleyado maapekto ham.

**[01:43:58.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000885)  
Na una, yung kanila pong retirement ay babayaran.

**[01:44:04.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000886)  
Pangalawa, bukod po sa regular retirement, magkakaroon po ng tinatawag na special incentive package.

**[01:44:12.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000887)  
Kung saan, because of the involuntary nature of the retirement,

**[01:44:18.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000888)  
magkakaroon po ng karagdagang retirement package aside from retirement.

**[01:44:23.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000889)  
Di normal retirement package ang isang maapekto hang empleyado ng PAGCOR.

**[01:44:30.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000890)  
Dito sa nasabing decoupling privatization na ito.

**[01:44:36.880] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000891)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman. Madam Chair, thank you very much.

**[01:44:41.840] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000892)  
At ang TJP Partylist, pakunguna po ni Vice Chairman ng Appropriation at ng Deputy Majority of Bung Tebes

**[01:44:50.560] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000893)  
ay palaging sumusuporta sa PAGCOR.

**[01:44:53.239] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000894)  
Thank you very much.

**[01:44:54.100] Rep. Jose "Bong" J. Teves Jr.** (asr-000895)  
Panaming salam.

**[01:44:55.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000896)  
Panaming salamat din po, Your Honor.

**[01:45:02.159] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000897)  
Thank you very much, Representative Bong Teves.

**[01:45:04.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-000898)  
We'll now move to our next interpellator from the minority, Representative Sarah Elago.

**[01:45:11.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000899)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Good morning.

**[01:45:14.599] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000900)  
A few clarificatory questions on the nationwide school building program,

**[01:45:18.639] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000901)  
notwithstanding, of course, the concerns raised by our fellow legislators.

**[01:45:24.280] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000902)  
The initial funding stated here is 1.37 billion pesos.

**[01:45:28.920] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000903)  
Is this for the year? O kasama na po yung for next year din?

**[01:45:38.679] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000904)  
Your Honor, baka po, anong page po ba yan?

**[01:45:43.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000905)  
Ganito po yan.

**[01:45:45.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000906)  
In our memorandum of agreement with the Department of Education

**[01:45:50.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000907)  
signed by myself and Secretary Sunny Angara,

**[01:45:55.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000908)  
we will be funding the construction of 125 buildings

**[01:46:02.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000909)  
and 3-story, 12-classroom school buildings,

**[01:46:08.446] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000910)  
the total amount of which,

**[01:46:11.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000911)  
including the equipment and furnitures like the chairs,

**[01:46:16.239] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000912)  
will be roughly about 7.5 billion pesos.

**[01:46:20.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000913)  
So that will be the equivalent of about 1,500 classrooms.

**[01:46:30.501] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000914)  
They are...

**[01:46:31.679] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000915)  
They will be equipped...

**[01:46:32.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000916)  
I mean, they will have furnitures already,

**[01:46:35.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000917)  
like the student chairs and tables.

**[01:46:38.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000918)  
They will have the 75-inch TVs in every classroom

**[01:46:44.119] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000919)  
and meron pong amplifier yung teacher and so on and so forth.

**[01:46:50.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000920)  
So that program, Your Honor, will be for the period 2026 up to the end of 2027.

**[01:47:00.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000921)  
We will be...

**[01:47:01.480] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000922)  
It will be the Department of Education that will be vetting and recommending

**[01:47:07.480] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000923)  
the school building that will be constructed

**[01:47:11.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000924)  
and it will be PAGCOR through the different LGUs that will be funding the construction.

**[01:47:18.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000925)  
So for this year, as the process has already started,

**[01:47:24.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000926)  
we have earmarked close to about 3.5 billion pesos

**[01:47:29.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000927)  
to pay for the construction of the school buildings.

**[01:47:30.480] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000928)  
For the 50% down payment that we will release

**[01:47:34.871] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000929)  
to the different local government units

**[01:47:38.239] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000930)  
and then there will be a series of progressive billing payments

**[01:47:43.159] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000931)  
that we will make dependent on the completion of every school building.

**[01:47:54.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000932)  
Yes, Madam Chair.

**[01:47:54.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000933)  
We'll just request PAGCOR to submit to this committee a breakdown

**[01:47:59.119] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000934)  
of the...

**[01:48:02.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000935)  
utilization of these funds for the nationwide school building program.

**[01:48:07.679] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000936)  
So malinaw po, no?

**[01:48:09.480] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000937)  
DepEd ang mag-be-vet, mag-identify, magkitiyak kung saan po mapupunta

**[01:48:14.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000938)  
itong mga classrooms na dagdag na nais nating magawa.

**[01:48:17.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000939)  
Maliwanag po yun, Your Honor.

**[01:48:19.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000940)  
At kanina ngayon may mga nagtatunong,

**[01:48:21.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000941)  
pati po sa bidding, wala pong pakialam ang PAGCOR.

**[01:48:26.360] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000942)  
Ang PAGCOR po ay magpo-pondo lamang.

**[01:48:30.377] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000943)  
There are so many requirements between the Department of Education

**[01:48:35.734] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000944)  
and the local government unit concerned.

**[01:48:39.883] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000945)  
PAGCOR basically collates all of them.

**[01:48:43.639] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000946)  
And between myself and the LGU or the district concerned,

**[01:48:52.360] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000947)  
meron po memorandum of agreement.

**[01:48:54.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000948)  
And then we shall release the first funds

**[01:48:59.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000949)  
of the budget to the respective LGUs who will take charge of the bidding process.

**[01:49:09.644] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000950)  
Okay, Chair.

**[01:49:09.952] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000951)  
Your Honor.

**[01:49:10.904] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000952)  
Yes.

**[01:49:11.520] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000953)  
Yung pondo po natin magagaling dito sa Fund for Social Civic Projects.

**[01:49:15.800] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000954)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:49:16.360] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000955)  
Is this correct?

**[01:49:17.199] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000956)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[01:49:18.566] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000957)  
Alright.

**[01:49:18.880] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000958)  
Now let me go to the...

**[01:49:21.080] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000959)  
I'd like to follow through on questions

**[01:49:24.320] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000960)  
with regard to the regulation on gambling ads.

**[01:49:26.599] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000961)  
Nabanggit niyo po kanina na meron,

**[01:49:28.239] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000962)  
kayong mga napatawa ng fines.

**[01:49:32.639] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000963)  
Nabanggit niyong halimbawa,

**[01:49:34.075] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000964)  
one million pesos.

**[01:49:35.040] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000965)  
Pero for the past months,

**[01:49:38.440] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000966)  
nasinimulan niyo po itong mas mahigpit ninyo na regulation,

**[01:49:41.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000967)  
magkano na yung buong nakolekta?

**[01:49:43.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000968)  
At saan yung ginagamit ito?

**[01:49:47.119] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000969)  
Ang nakolekta na po namin since 2026,

**[01:49:51.719] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000970)  
meron na po sigurong mayigit isandang milyong piso.

**[01:49:56.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000971)  
Ito po, it forms part of the other income.

**[01:49:58.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000972)  
As stated in our statement.

**[01:50:02.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000973)  
At yan po ay nasasama po sa corporate income natin.

**[01:50:09.000] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000974)  
Kung saan that is distributed as listed in our presentation, Your Honor.

**[01:50:15.679] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000975)  
Nabanggit niyo rin kanina na meron na tayong mga nakasuhan.

**[01:50:21.159] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000976)  
Pwede ba kaming humingi ng listahan ng mga nakasuhan niyo na

**[01:50:25.159] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000977)  
at kung meron na bang nanagot,

**[01:50:27.599] Rep. Sarah Jane I. Elago** (asr-000978)  
meron na bang nasuspindi,

**[01:50:29.239] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000979)  
meron na bang nakansela ang license dahil dito sa stricter rules ninyo?

**[01:50:35.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000980)  
Your Honor, binanggit ko po kanina,

**[01:50:39.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000981)  
yung nakasuhan ay hindi po nagmula sa PAGCOR.

**[01:50:44.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000982)  
Ang nagkaso po laban dito sa mga nababanggit kanina pang mga endorsers or influencers

**[01:50:51.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000983)  
ay nagmula po sa CICC.

**[01:50:54.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000984)  
Kami po ay naguulat sa kanila,

**[01:50:57.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000985)  
at sila po ang nagpa-file ng kaso laban sa mga influencers na ito.

**[01:51:06.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000986)  
Ang pagkakaalam ko po, halos simigit kumulang po yata yung labing dalawa or labing lima na yung na-file na nila ng kaso.

**[01:51:13.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000987)  
So, sa inyong kahilingan, Your Honor,

**[01:51:16.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000988)  
hihingi po kami ng listahan mula sa CICC

**[01:51:21.560] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000989)  
which we can forward to the Committee on Appropriations.

**[01:51:25.480] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000990)  
So, all the members are updated.

**[01:51:27.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000991)  
Dito po sa isinasagawang operasyon, laban po dito sa mga endorsers na ito.

**[01:51:34.796] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000992)  
Alright.

**[01:51:35.679] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000993)  
Meron bang mas pinalakas din na hakbang para habulin naman itong foreign gambling companies na illegal ho na nag-operate dito?

**[01:51:44.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000994)  
Kasi you mentioned only for the local endorsers, influencers, but what about those companies?

**[01:51:52.360] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000995)  
Tulad po ng nabanggit ko kanina, Your Honor, wala po kaming police powers.

**[01:51:58.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000996)  
So, we are dependent on DICT, CICC, and NTC.

**[01:52:04.639] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000997)  
Sila po, alam ko eh, nakipag-ugnayan na rin sa lahat ng social media platforms.

**[01:52:11.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-000998)  
Sumulat na po sila sa mga pinuno nito para ipagbigay alam na hindi pinahintulutan dito sa ating bansa yung mga nasabing gawain na yon.

**[02:02:23.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001170)

**[02:02:30.910] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001171)  
una, malakas po ang programa namin ngayon sa paghihikayat sa mga iligal na operator

**[02:02:39.303] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001172)  
na magpalisensya na.

**[02:02:41.950] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001173)  
Dahil ang paniwala ko po sa pangkasalukuyan sa industriya,

**[02:02:47.200] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001174)  
50% po ang may lisensya,

**[02:02:54.035] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001175)  
50% din po ang walang lisensya.

**[02:02:55.807] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001176)  
So, very positive naman, Your Honor, ang aming programa

**[02:03:00.835] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001177)  
sapagkat meron na po kaming mga natatanggap ng mga feelers

**[02:03:05.790] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001178)  
para mag-surrender na ang ilang malalaking iligal na magpalisensya na po sa pangkasalukuyan.

**[02:03:12.344] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001179)  
So, number one po yun.

**[02:03:15.669] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001180)  
Number two, tulad nung binanggit ko kanina sa katanungan ni Congressman Odi earlier,

**[02:03:29.069] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001181)  
aming pong minomonitor ng husto ang income na or GGR or operations ngayon

**[02:03:36.270] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001182)  
ng mga online gaming companies sa pamamagitan ng isang third-party auditor.

**[02:03:42.179] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001183)  
Doon po ay aming nakikita kung tunay pang nairimit nila or dinideklara nila yung tama nilang gross gaming revenue.

**[02:03:52.861] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001184)  
At gusto ko pong ibalita sa inyo,

**[02:03:57.502] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001185)  
malaki na rin po ang naging pagtaas ng mga gross gaming revenue ng online gaming operators

**[02:04:05.790] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001186)  
dahil naalaman nila at nakikita nilang nakatutok ang isang third-party auditor.

**[02:04:16.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001187)  
At panghuli po ay nag-ikayat kami na sana'y marami pang mag-apply dito sa special class BPO

**[02:04:26.730] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001188)  
kung saan po yung mga nasa ibang bansa na mga malalaking operator ay gamitin na ang Pilipinas

**[02:04:35.673] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001189)  
bilang kanilang mga backroom operations ay dito na gawin sa Maynila.

**[02:04:44.887] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001190)  
Very encouraging, Your Honor, Kong Ador, itong binabanggit ko sapagkat sa ngayon po eh,

**[02:04:52.709] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001191)  
nasa sa nung linunsad po namin yung programa, two-fold po,

**[02:04:55.062] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001192)  
bukod sa may karagdagang kita sa PAGCOR, may himigit kumulang po sa 7,500 empleyado na

**[02:05:07.567] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001193)  
ang nasabing sektor nito na hindi nagbabayad ng minimum wage,

**[02:05:13.790] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001194)  
kundi nagbabayad ng mga rates ng customer service agents ng mga BPO.

**[02:05:19.651] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001195)  
So this amounts to an average of...

**[02:05:23.969] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001196)  
35,000 to 45,000 pesos a month.

**[02:05:26.510] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001197)  
So yun po ang aming ginagawa sa pangkasulukuyan,

**[02:05:29.538] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001198)  
and we're also hoping, Your Honor, Kong Ador,

**[02:05:34.111] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001199)  
na dumating yung pagkakataon na matapos na rin yung crisis sa Middle East,

**[02:05:38.937] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001200)  
nang sa ganun eh, medyo pag nakaluwag-luwag naman sila eh,

**[02:05:44.079] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001201)  
ang tao eh, marami na rin ang magpe-patronize ng ating mga naroon.

**[02:05:46.531] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001202)  
Thank you, Chairman Al, for that response.

**[02:05:57.348] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001203)  
Specifically, what is PAGCOR's strategy in terms of improving its regulatory framework,

**[02:06:04.047] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001204)  
expanding the legitimate gaming market, strengthening its digital monitoring and collection system,

**[02:06:09.790] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001205)  
and addressing illegal and unlicensed operators,

**[02:06:15.033] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001206)  
and also attracting responsible investment into the Philippine gaming industry.

**[02:06:20.146] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001207)  
Okay.

**[02:06:23.500] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001208)  
Doon po sa katanungan ninyo, Your Honor, una,

**[02:06:28.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001209)  
sa structure po, wala pong sawa kaming nagre-review ng aming structures and guidelines regularly

**[02:06:38.185] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001210)  
para nang sa ganun ma-improve ito.

**[02:06:43.467] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001211)  
Yan po ay patutunayan ng updated structure ng online gaming rules ng PAGCOR.

**[02:06:56.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001212)  
Halos po buwan-buwan, meron po kaming mga adjustments na ginagawa

**[02:07:00.730] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001213)  
para ito po ay matama at maging ma-responsive sa pangkasulukuyang sitwasyon.

**[02:07:11.530] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001214)  
Number two, kami po ay patuloy na dumalabas at nagsasabi,

**[02:07:20.990] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001215)  
nag-a-attend sa mga konbensyon, ngayon din naman ang mga exhibits abroad,

**[02:07:30.002] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001216)  
at nagsasabing bukas ang Pilipinas sa paglilesensya ng land-based casinos,

**[02:07:33.738] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001217)  
ngayon din naman ng ating online gaming.

**[02:07:40.870] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001218)  
Hindi man dapat sabihin, pero pwede na rin ipagmalaki na dito po sa Asia,

**[02:07:45.213] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001219)  
ay next to Macau.

**[02:07:53.414] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001220)  
Halos we are competing.

**[02:07:55.490] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001221)  
Head to head na with Singapore.

**[02:07:56.975] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001222)  
And I believe, malaking bagay po itong darating na privatization,

**[02:08:02.240] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001223)  
sapagkat pagdakailam na po at ang nagpatakbo na po ng mga kasino

**[02:08:06.982] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001224)  
na sa pangkasulukuyan ay pinapatakbo ng PAGCOR,

**[02:08:10.510] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001225)  
naniniwala po ko it will be a game changer.

**[02:08:15.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001226)  
Sapagkat pag ang pribadong sektor na ang nagpapatakbo ng negosyo,

**[02:08:19.958] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001227)  
iba na po ito eh.

**[02:08:23.485] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001228)  
Eh sa pangkasulukuyan, your honor, we cannot compete head on with our licenses.

**[02:08:32.229] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001229)  
Yung kasino Filipino, your honor, wala man lang kaming kwarto na mga hotel na inalok.

**[02:08:39.432] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001230)  
Wala kaming mga restaurants, wala kaming mga mall, wala kaming mga prebileyong maipagkaloob.

**[02:08:44.819] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001231)  
Dahil wala namang yung mga properties natin.

**[02:08:47.792] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001232)  
Na ganitong facilities.

**[02:08:53.950] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001233)  
Pero pag ang kasino Filipino po at itong mga properties natin ay na-privatize na,

**[02:09:00.819] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001234)  
naniniwala po ako, yung bibili ng pribadong kumpanya,

**[02:09:03.750] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001235)  
eh magagawa na ng hakbang yan para lumakas ang kanilang kasino.

**[02:09:09.672] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001236)  
At pag lumakas ang kasino, sapagkat tayo ay mga umalik tala lang ng license fees,

**[02:09:15.663] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001237)  
tatas din rin automatic ang income ng PAGCOR.

**[02:09:19.549] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001238)  
Kaya yan po ang mga pangkasulukuyang mga ginagawa namin.

**[02:09:26.950] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001239)  
At hopefully nga, pagka ito pong decoupling privatization ay maganap na,

**[02:09:29.300] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001240)  
malaki po ang magiging epekto nito sa kita ng PAGCOR.

**[02:09:34.394] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001241)  
Dagdag pa ron, Congressman Pleyto,

**[02:09:41.397] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001242)  
eh masakit man sabihin, ang average po namin na pasweldo sa tao ay mahigit isang billion piso sa isang buwan.

**[02:09:50.712] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001243)  
Tapos ang average na binabayad namin sa upa ng mga kasino properties namin,

**[02:09:55.350] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001244)  
mahigit 250 million piso isang buwan.

**[02:09:58.981] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001245)  
Eh yun lang po, maalis natin sa corporate expense ng PAGCOR,

**[02:10:05.377] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001246)  
na parang naking bagay na po na ito para lang sa ganun,

**[02:10:13.910] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001247)  
maitulong yan ng PAGCOR sa nation building at sa ating kapwa mamamayang Pilipino.

**[02:10:18.927] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001248)  
Maraming salamat, Chairman Al.

**[02:10:21.813] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001249)  
Salamat po naman, Your Honor.

**[02:10:23.011] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001250)  
At Madam Chair, pwede ba yung remaining time ko,

**[02:10:25.976] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001251)  
bigay ko sa aking kaibigan, kay Congressman Dumugan,

**[02:10:29.963] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001252)  
kasi napakarami namang nangyaring mga nasirang infrastruktura doon sa Koldilyera,

**[02:10:37.912] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001253)  
lalo ng Baguio-Binguet, sa infrastructure, saka agriculture.

**[02:10:42.907] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001254)  
Ito po, Mr. Chair, Madam Chair, I would like to place on record,

**[02:10:48.589] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001255)  
that despite the significant decline in its gross gaming revenues and net income,

**[02:10:54.109] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001256)  
PAGCOR has continued to fulfill its social responsibility.

**[02:10:57.349] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001257)  
Gusto ko pa sa salamatan ng mga taga PAGCOR.

**[02:11:01.030] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001258)  
May sincere appreciation yung binanggit kanina ni Assistant President Balcos, si Eric,

**[02:11:08.236] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001259)  
tungkol po doon sa JBL, Children's Medical Center, in Clark, Pangpanga,

**[02:11:13.189] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001260)  
which was recently inaugurated by our beloved President Bongbong Marcos.

**[02:11:19.154] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001261)  
Ito po.

**[02:11:21.483] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001262)  
Ito po ay this very important investment for those children needing specialized medical care.

**[02:11:24.250] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001263)  
Napakaganda po ito.

**[02:11:26.444] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001264)  
Salamat kami mga taga Central Luzon, lalo na ang Bulacan, malapit doon,

**[02:11:30.342] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001265)  
at yung mga taga Central Luzon.

**[02:11:32.367] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001266)  
Saka lahat po tayo, kasi para sa mga batang mga isakit,

**[02:11:38.870] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001267)  
napakaganda hindi na tayo pupunta ng Manila, doon na lang tayo sa Clark.

**[02:11:44.310] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001268)  
We've also seen PAGCOR.

**[02:11:46.042] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001269)  
Ito ay totoo po naman po ito.

**[02:11:47.062] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001270)  
Yung mga pangangailangan sa Mindanao,

**[02:11:50.103] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001271)  
nung nangyari yung mga typhoons and earthquakes, recent earthquakes in Mindanao,

**[02:11:52.533] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001272)  
itong mga typhoons na nangyari dahil sa monsoon, south-west monsoon, itong habagat,

**[02:11:59.924] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001273)  
alam ko ang PAGCOR, they continue to affect many parts.

**[02:12:06.809] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001274)  
PAGCOR once again stands ready to provide assistance in our disaster-stricken communities.

**[02:12:11.467] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001275)  
At the time, Madam Chair, when government resources are stretched

**[02:12:16.990] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001276)  
by the increasing free...

**[02:12:19.041] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001277)  
frequency and severity of natural disasters, this commitment is truly commendable.

**[02:12:27.109] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001278)  
It demonstrates that PAGCOR's role goes beyond gaming and revenue generation.

**[02:12:31.936] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001279)  
It is also an institution that contributes to nation building, ang sabi mo kanina, and

**[02:12:36.719] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001280)  
provides meaningful support when our people need it most, especially those who have lost

**[02:12:41.639] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001281)  
their homes, livelihoods, and sense of security because of the calamities.

**[02:12:43.960] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001282)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:12:44.760] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001283)  
Thank you, Chairman Al.

**[02:12:48.052] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001284)  
Thank you, Representative Pleyto.

**[02:12:50.212] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001285)  
Chair Al, would you like to respond?

**[02:12:53.249] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001286)  
Okay po.

**[02:12:55.190] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001287)  
Maraming salamat din po, Congressman Pleyto, sa inyong mga binanggit.

**[02:13:02.986] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001288)  
Makakaasa po kayo na patuloy pong kasama ninyo ang PAGCOR sa pakikipagtulungan sa ating mga kababayan.

**[02:13:09.095] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001289)  
Salamat po.

**[02:13:09.972] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001290)  
Maraming salamat.

**[02:13:11.491] Rep. Salvador A. Pleyto** (asr-001291)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[02:13:12.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001292)  
Thank you, Representative Pleyto.

**[02:13:14.199] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001293)  
We'll now move to our next interpellator from the minority.

**[02:13:18.400] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-001294)  
Representative Iris Marie Montes.

**[02:13:20.000] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001295)  
Thank you, Madam Chair. Magandang umaga po sa ating lahat.

**[02:13:25.159] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001296)  
Mr. Chair, Madam Chair, RA 11223 or the Universal Health Care Act was enacted on February 20, 2019.

**[02:13:36.480] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001297)  
Section 37B of that law provides that 50% of the 50% national government share from the income of PAGCOR will be used by PhilHealth for its benefit package.

**[02:13:53.960] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001298)  
Also in the same law, it provided that remittance from PAGCOR should have started in 2020 or one year after the enactment of the law.

**[02:14:06.119] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001299)  
My first question is, was PAGCOR able to comply with this provision?

**[02:14:10.400] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001300)  
Kailan po kayo nag-umpisang mag-remit sa National Treasury for UHC?

**[02:14:22.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001301)  
Madam Chairperson, I'd like to answer.

**[02:14:25.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001302)  
Your Honor, from the implementation of the said Republic Act, PAGCOR has religiously remitted its shares to the national government.

**[02:14:40.212] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001303)  
Now, as I had also clarified this in one of the hearings in the Senate, it is not the responsibility of PAGCOR to remit directly to PhilHealth.

**[02:14:51.597] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001304)  
Yes.

**[02:14:52.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001305)  
Because the law states...

**[02:14:52.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001306)  
states that we remit

**[02:14:55.400] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001307)  
and then it is the

**[02:14:56.719] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001308)  
national government that will remit

**[02:14:59.280] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001309)  
50% of what

**[02:15:00.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001310)  
we attributed to

**[02:15:02.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001311)  
the PhilHealth.

**[02:15:05.440] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001312)  
In answer to your question,

**[02:15:07.320] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001313)  
Your Honor, yes,

**[02:15:10.719] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001314)  
PAGCOR has

**[02:15:11.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001315)  
remitted religiously

**[02:15:13.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001316)  
and as of

**[02:15:15.320] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001317)  
today, we are current

**[02:15:17.599] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001318)  
and wala po kaming

**[02:15:18.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001319)  
pagkukulang noong sa nasabing

**[02:15:20.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001320)  
remittance na iyo.

**[02:15:22.360] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001321)  
Maraming salamat po. So, religiously

**[02:15:24.719] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001322)  
meaning compliant with the

**[02:15:26.880] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001323)  
provision of the law one year after its

**[02:15:28.440] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001324)  
enactment. So, I suppose it's 2020

**[02:15:30.760] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001325)  
ano po. Based kasi po

**[02:15:33.079] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001326)  
dito sa inyong presentation

**[02:15:34.719] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001327)  
handout, tinignan nyo

**[02:15:36.920] Rep. Iris Marie Demesa Montes** (asr-001328)

**[02:27:26.600] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001679)  
So, good morning. May mga konting katanungan lang po, Madam Chair, sa ating PAGCOR. Una po yung ating displacement and mass layoff from casino privatization.

**[02:27:40.640] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001680)  
So, PAGCOR plans to privatize around 40 casino Filipino properties, potentially affecting approximately 10,000 regular and contractual employees.

**[02:27:52.800] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001681)  
Since asset sale ang structure, hindi automatic na required ang private buyers na i-retain ang existing workforce.

**[02:28:01.920] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001682)  
May proposed 50-70% employee absorption requirement, pero maaari itong makapektos sa valuation at bids dahil sa additional labor costs and liabilities.

**[02:28:13.160] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001683)  
Ang katanungan ko po sa PAGCOR, Madam Chair, is sa privatization ng said 40 casino Filipino properties.

**[02:28:23.080] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001684)  
Ilan po sa approximately 10,000 employees ang projected na ma-displace or ma-retrench?

**[02:28:30.960] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001685)  
At kung ang proposed 50-70% employee absorption requirement ay mag-resulta sa lower bids, ano ang magiging priority ng PAGCOR?

**[02:28:41.640] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001686)  
Maximizing privatization proceeds or protecting existing jobs?

**[02:28:46.040] Rep. Jan "Atty. JP" Padiernos** (asr-001687)  
And ano po ang konkretong safeguards para sa mga employees na hindi ma-absorb?

**[02:28:51.479] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001688)  
Okay.

**[02:28:52.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001689)

**[02:29:00.279] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-001690)

about the gaming

**[02:59:30.961] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002767)  
Ito,

is it about

**[02:59:33.253] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002769)  
illegal

**[02:59:34.046] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002770)  
mga online?

**[02:59:35.177] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002771)  
Your Honor,

**[02:59:36.449] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002772)  
Congressman de Jesus,

**[02:59:39.503] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002773)  
yun po yung

**[02:59:40.435] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002774)

**[02:59:44.422] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002777)  
When you have
