to operate

**[02:59:48.323] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002780)  
an online

**[02:59:49.537] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002781)  
gaming

**[02:59:50.415] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002782)  
company,

**[02:59:52.421] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002783)  
you are

**[02:59:53.382] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002784)  
considered

**[02:59:54.231] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002785)  
as a

**[02:59:55.643] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002788)  
either as a GSA or Gaming Service Administrator.

**[02:59:59.926] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002789)  
So, how many of them, Madam Chair?

**[03:00:02.132] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002790)  
Today, Your Honor, at its peak, we had 74.

**[03:00:07.623] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002791)  
But as of today, we are down to 48, Your Honor.

**[03:00:13.899] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002792)  
What happened? May violations nila? Suspended?

**[03:00:17.249] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002793)  
Meron na pong suspended, meron na pong natanggalan ng lisensya,

**[03:00:21.638] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002794)  
at meron na rin pong nag-surrender ng kanilang lisensya, Your Honor.

**[03:00:27.025] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002795)  
Are they local o may foreign din bang mga?

**[03:00:30.565] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002796)  
Yung lahat pong nangyon, mga 98% po are local.

**[03:00:39.493] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002797)  
So, local. So, what's the reason why they surrendered? Because they're losing money?

**[03:00:43.457] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002798)  
They surrendered because they're losing money, yes.

**[03:00:46.956] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002799)  
For your information, Your Honor,

**[03:00:50.200] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002800)  
Congressman De Jesus,

**[03:00:58.681] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002801)  
kung saan hindi na po pwede gawain nagpaparada ka lang ng lisensya

**[03:01:04.343] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002802)  
or wala kang ginagawa sa lisensya mo, effective po nung July 1.

**[03:01:09.348] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002803)  
Meron na pong minimum na 9 million pesos na minimum guarantee fee

**[03:01:17.321] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002804)  
ang ini-impose ng PAGCOR.

**[03:01:19.569] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002805)  
And then, ang BIR naman po may karagdagang 5%.

**[03:01:23.619] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002806)  
So, kung ikaw po ay isang GSA, meron ka na pong minimum na dapat bayaran na 10.5 million pesos.

**[03:01:32.375] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002807)  
9 million sa PAGCOR and 1.5 million po sa Bureau of Internal Revenue.

**[03:01:37.931] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002808)  
So, yung mga dormant po or yung mga nag-iintay lang na may balak na magbenta or whatever,

**[03:01:45.311] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002809)  
eh wala namang activity pa sa pangkasalukuyan, napakabigat po.

**[03:01:49.826] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002810)  
Kung sila yung maglalabas ng 10.5 million pesos sa month,

**[03:01:53.939] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002811)  
meron na rin pong ilan na nag-surrender na po ng kanilang lisensya.

**[03:01:59.324] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002812)  
And this for me is a very good program para po ma-rationalize

**[03:02:10.222] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002813)  
tsaka ma-stabilize na natin yung online gaming industry natin.

**[03:02:15.249] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002814)  
You answered my next question about those mga dormant, no?

**[03:02:19.256] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002815)  
Kasi walang pinagkaiba yan.

**[03:02:20.613] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002816)  
Sa Department of Energy, tinatawag namin service contract.

**[03:02:23.836] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002817)  
Bibili lang sila, maghihintay sila ng buyer.

**[03:02:26.784] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002818)  
So, in your case, wala nang ganun.

**[03:02:28.373] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002819)  
You can assure ya, Chairman?

**[03:02:30.155] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002820)  
Well, Your Honor, Congressman, I cannot claim that hindi na po mangyayari.

**[03:02:37.004] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002821)  
Pero ang malaking deterrent po talaga eh yung namumuhunan sila ng karagdagan.

**[03:02:42.347] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002822)  
10.5 ng 10.5 billion, medyo mabigat din po yun kung talagang wala kayong ginagawa.

**[03:02:47.562] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002823)  
At ang totoo po,

**[03:02:48.623] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002824)  
naging feedback po namin bigla ngayon, wala na po halos nagbebenta.

**[03:02:52.778] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002825)  
Magsusurrender na lang po sila ng lisensya.

**[03:02:55.387] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002826)  
So, yan po ay isang programang ipinatupad po ng inyong lingkod nitong ika-isa ng Hulyo nitong taong ito.

**[03:03:05.628] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002827)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:03:06.456] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002828)  
Going to my next question.

**[03:03:08.363] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002829)  
Under your corporate mandate, nakalagay dito, number one,

**[03:03:12.138] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002830)  
regulate, operate, authorize, and license games of chance.

**[03:03:17.842] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002831)  
Cards and numbers, particularly casino gaming in the Philippines.

**[03:03:22.760] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002832)  
So, ano itong, when you say games of chance, ano mga under neto?

**[03:03:26.007] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002833)  
Well, ang totoo po, ang na-under lang dyan, na inabutan ko na po,

**[03:03:31.964] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002834)  
ay yun talagang casino games, tsaka po itong online gaming.

**[03:03:36.099] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002835)  
Pero pagdating po sa horse racing, under po yan sa gab.

**[03:03:41.656] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002836)  
Cock fighting, under po yan sa gab.

**[03:03:43.881] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002837)  
Yung isa po palang nawala rin.

**[03:03:46.766] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002838)  
Namin, nadating nan dyan, ay yung pong online sabong.

**[03:03:50.794] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002839)  
Halimbawa, so games of chance po na-classify, pero nawala na rin po yan.

**[03:03:55.989] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002840)  
So, sa pangkasalukuyan po, yung in reference to games of chance,

**[03:04:02.138] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002841)  
ang tinutukoy po dyan ay mga casino games at yung online gaming games.

**[03:04:08.923] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002842)  
So, casino games and online gaming.

**[03:04:11.954] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002843)  
Hindi yung kagay sinabi mo yung horse racing, that's under gab.

**[03:04:15.560] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002844)  
Hindi po.

**[03:04:15.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002845)  
Nasa gab po ang horse racing, tsaka meron po yung Philippine Racing Commission.

**[03:04:20.166] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002846)  
Tapos, yung pong ibang cock fighting, sa gab din po yan, wala po sa PAGCOR.

**[03:04:26.599] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002847)  
Okay. So, anything, basta sa inyo, online.

**[03:04:29.462] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002848)  
So, we need to depreciate.

**[03:04:30.864] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002849)  
Online and yung pong games of chance, yung casino games.

**[03:04:32.629] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002850)  
Yung pong nakikita natin sa mga land-based casinos natin.

**[03:04:36.567] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002851)  
So, anything land-based casino, under casino property nila,

**[03:04:41.743] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002852)  
plus online games.

**[03:04:43.062] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002853)  
So, online gaming is under PAGCOR.

**[03:04:45.600] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002854)  
Tama ba yan?

**[03:04:46.146] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002855)  
Yes.

**[03:04:46.738] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002856)  
Your Honor, tama po kayo.

**[03:04:48.625] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002857)  
Okay. Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:04:50.637] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002858)  
And again, I would like to reiterate my point that I raised a clarificatory question ago

**[03:04:55.932] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002859)  
about the budget, no, that being taken away from PAGCOR

**[03:05:01.920] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002860)

**[03:05:07.096] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002861)  
Kasi ang dami nyo natutulungan, sa totoo lang.

**[03:05:09.877] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002862)  
Again, thank you.

**[03:05:10.810] Rep. Presley C. De Jesus** (asr-002863)

**[03:05:52.239] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002876)  
So, first and foremost, Madam Chair, I just want to thank the PAGCOR chairman

**[03:05:57.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002877)  
because immediately after our previous hearing,

**[03:06:00.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002878)

**[03:06:07.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002879)

**[03:06:14.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002880)

**[03:06:15.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002881)  
I have a list here of the websites which you can still access via the computer.

**[03:06:21.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002882)  
My team is pulling it up on the laptop so that we can show it to them.

**[03:06:24.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002883)

**[03:06:32.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002884)  
Aside from that, I also want to point out that PAGCOR, if I'm not mistaken,

**[03:06:39.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002885)  
your GRR or Gross Gaming Revenues in 2025,

**[03:06:45.319] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002886)  
is up 6.39% from $372 billion in 2024.

**[03:06:54.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002887)  
So, how much are we looking at in terms of GGR for 2025 again?

**[03:06:59.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002888)  
I think it was mentioned earlier.

**[03:07:00.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002889)  
Are you, Your Honor, are you referring to 2025 or 2026?

**[03:07:06.120] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002890)  
For 2026.

**[03:07:08.200] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002891)  
For 2026, there is already a decline for both land-based casinos and online gaming.

**[03:07:15.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002892)  
We are looking at about P370 billion pesos.

**[03:07:22.517] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002893)  
P370 billion pesos.

**[03:07:24.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002894)  
More or less?

**[03:07:26.760] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002895)  
Sige po.

**[03:07:27.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002896)  
If we're looking at P370 billion pesos,

**[03:07:32.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002897)  
we're looking at how much in terms of the annual wagering turnover for 2026.

**[03:07:42.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002898)  
So, kumbaga, the gross revenue po that we,

**[03:07:45.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002899)  
are able to get is, yes, sir.

**[03:07:49.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002900)  
As, Your Honor, as gross gaming revenue is defined by total bets, less payouts.

**[03:07:56.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002901)  
So, maybe I should say the total wages placed will be in the vicinity of about P3 trillion.

**[03:08:12.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002902)  
About P3 trillion pesos, no?

**[03:08:15.399] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002903)  
Okay.

**[03:08:15.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002904)  
The reason why I bring this up, Madam Chair,

**[03:08:18.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002905)  
is because if there's about P3 trillion pesos that are being wagered per annum,

**[03:08:25.680] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002906)  
and we're getting about P300 plus billion in gross revenue from this,

**[03:08:31.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002907)  
can you imagine how much of the undeclared revenue

**[03:08:35.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002908)  
or how much of the undeclared annual wagers we're missing out on in terms of government revenue,

**[03:08:41.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002909)  
which could go towards government projects, government services?

**[03:08:45.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002910)  
And if we look here, for example,

**[03:08:48.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002911)  
I just pulled up PopoLive, which is accessible online again.

**[03:08:54.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002912)  
Madam Chair, may I ask if PopoLive and these other apps,

**[03:08:58.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002913)  
sorry, these other apps and websites from last year were able to secure a gambling license from PAGCOR?

**[03:09:06.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002914)  
Your Honor, I believe we have not issued a license,

**[03:09:13.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002915)  
primarily because we have, since last two years,

**[03:09:20.560] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002916)  
had a moratorium on licenses.

**[03:09:24.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002917)  
So, I am 100% sure they remain to be part of the illegal operators operating in the Philippines.

**[03:09:34.567] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002918)  
Madam Chair, so...

**[03:09:35.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002919)  
Your Honor, if you don't mind, I just want to say that

**[03:09:40.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002920)  
I'd like to thank you for recognizing the fact that

**[03:09:44.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002921)  
when you brought out the name,

**[03:09:48.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002922)  
the names of the illegal operators last year,

**[03:09:53.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002923)  
they were pulled down or put up.

**[03:09:56.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002924)  
That was not because of us.

**[03:09:59.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002925)  
We did make the recommendation to the DICT, CICC, and the NTC.

**[03:10:09.639] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002926)  
Because they are, Your Honor, the ones with the police powers.

**[03:10:15.917] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002927)  
They are the ones who have the power.

**[03:10:17.987] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002928)  
We are not...

**[03:10:19.350] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002929)  
There is nothing to be done but to make a recommendation,

**[03:10:21.090] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002930)  
to make a recommendation, and to make a recommendation only.

**[03:10:23.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002931)  
So, I just want to give credit where credit is due.

**[03:10:27.587] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002932)  
Because we are the ones who just recommended and just said

**[03:10:31.572] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002933)  
here in these government agencies that these are illegal and their operations must continue.

**[03:10:38.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002934)  
Madam Chair, I'd like to thank the PAGCOR chairman for clarifying that until now,

**[03:10:45.399] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002935)  
these apps and these gaming sites do not have a license.

**[03:10:48.279] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002936)  
Madam Chair, may I ask kung kasama po sa GGR ang revenue galing sa mga apps

**[03:10:55.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002937)  
or yung mga wagers placed on any of these websites or apps?

**[03:11:01.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002938)  
Your Honor, hindi po kasama.

**[03:11:02.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002939)  
We only track the GGR of our legal operators or our PAGCOR licenses.

**[03:11:12.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002940)  
Madam Chair, I bring this up kasi kung tutuusin itong mga sites na ito,

**[03:11:16.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002941)  
hindi sila kasama sa GGR,

**[03:11:18.200] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002942)  
yung GGR natin is already in the billions.

**[03:11:21.239] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002943)  
The total annual wager tracked is estimated at around 3 trillion pesos.

**[03:11:26.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002944)  
What more if these apps, these websites were properly tracked or suspended?

**[03:11:32.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002945)  
Kasi kung suspended po sila, then they will only gamble through the legal websites.

**[03:11:36.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002946)  
Tama po ba?

**[03:11:38.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002947)  
So, Mr. Chair, Madam Chair...

**[03:11:41.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002948)  
Yes, you are right, Your Honor.

**[03:11:43.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002949)  
Yun po ang sinasabi ko kanina,

**[03:11:46.200] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002950)  
na 50% lang po ng industriya ang may lisensya,

**[03:11:51.560] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002951)  
50% po ay walang lisensya.

**[03:11:54.279] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002952)  
You can just imagine, Your Honor, Congressman po,

**[03:11:59.120] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002953)  
what government is losing in terms of potential revenue.

**[03:12:03.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002954)  
Kaya talagang, dapat talagang pagtulungan na ating masugpo itong mga illegal na ito.

**[03:12:10.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002955)  
Malaki po, nawawala sa atin lahat dyan sa nangyayaring yan, Your Honor.

**[03:12:15.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002956)  
Madam Chair, what's important here is during this committee hearing,

**[03:12:18.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002957)  
nabanggit po ni Chairman that wala pang na-issue na bago na lisensya because there's a moratorium.

**[03:12:26.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002958)  
50% lang ang nakukuha natin na revenue.

**[03:12:30.399] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002959)  
Instead, we could be doubling the government revenue that we could possibly get from this to put into programs.

**[03:12:36.680] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002960)  
Madam Chair, moving on quickly to my next set of questions.

**[03:12:40.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002961)  
Madam Chair, PAGCOR pointed out that there are many,

**[03:12:43.439] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002962)  
there are many nation building programs.

**[03:12:45.840] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002963)  
Kumbaga, they have the school building program,

**[03:12:49.000] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002964)  
they have the patient transport vehicles which they have donated,

**[03:12:53.040] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002965)  
relief ops that they have conducted,

**[03:12:55.479] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002966)  
and other, a medical center even that they've inaugurated.

**[03:13:00.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002967)  
Pero gusto kong magtanong kung meron po silang donations or funding po para sa mental health services para sa mga gambling addicts po.

**[03:13:12.279] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002968)  
Kasi kung tutuusin itong mga working class citizens po natin na nagiging biktima po ng online gaming

**[03:13:19.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002969)  
and naging addict po,

**[03:13:21.720] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002970)  
naghahanap ng solusyon ng paraan para iwasan itong gambling habit nila.

**[03:13:28.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002971)  
And I was hoping that PAGCOR might have some mental health programs that they are funding or that they are supporting

**[03:13:37.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002972)  
so that it could be free for our constituents

**[03:13:40.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002973)  
who can't afford mental health services.

**[03:13:43.479] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002974)  
Meron po ba, Mr. Chair?

**[03:13:46.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002975)  
Your Honor, Congressman po.

**[03:13:49.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002976)  
That is part of our responsible gaming programs.

**[03:13:55.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002977)  
As I had mentioned, we have our partner, Seagulls Flock Foundation.

**[03:13:59.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002978)  
And sila po ay, sila ang nagpapatakbo ng 24-7 call center na sumasagot sa mga may problema

**[03:14:09.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002979)  
sa paglalaro.

**[03:14:11.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002980)  
At tulad ng ipinangako ko kanina, magsusumiti po kami ng report sa committee nito

**[03:14:19.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002981)  
para mapakita kung ilan na po yung ating mga natulungan tungkol sa mga himihingi ng tulong dahil sila ay,

**[03:14:29.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002982)  
meron na, nalululunan sila sa pagsusugal.

**[03:14:33.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002983)  
We will do that and we will submit to the committee.

**[03:14:38.279] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002984)  
So that it can be disseminated to all our, to the members of the committee.

**[03:14:44.800] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002985)  
Madam Chair, I'd like to request for a copy of those programs, the number of people.

**[03:14:49.479] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002986)  
the number of people who have been helped, how much funding has gone into it,

**[03:14:53.439] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002987)  
kasi importante at mahalaga po ito.

**[03:14:55.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002988)  
Tulad ng sinabi ni Rep. Elie earlier,

**[03:14:58.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002989)  
we do have a gambling addiction problem here in this country that we want to address.

**[03:15:04.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002990)  
We want it to be responsible, we want it to be well-managed,

**[03:15:07.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002991)  
but we also want people to have options para kung gusto nila tigilin tong habit na to,

**[03:15:13.479] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002992)  
pwede naman sila mag-seek ng help galing sa gobyerno po.

**[03:15:18.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002993)  
So, further, I just want to ask also, Madam Chair,

**[03:15:21.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002994)  
if PAGCOR, given all of its efforts with law enforcement,

**[03:15:27.600] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002995)  
is only able to address 50% of the gambling that takes place in this country,

**[03:15:33.439] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-002996)  
what programs or what, I guess, facilities in CICC, DICT,

**[03:15:41.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002997)  
can we add funding to or can we strengthen

**[03:15:45.248] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002998)  
in order to...

**[03:15:46.160] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-002999)  
in order for us to address all this illegal online gaming?

**[03:15:52.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003000)  
Your Honor, in our discussions since last year with DICT,

**[03:15:58.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003001)  
headed by Secretary Henry Aguda,

**[03:16:01.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003002)  
and CICC, headed by ASEC Aboy Paraíso,

**[03:16:07.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003003)  
there are certain tools that they need to procure.

**[03:16:13.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003004)  
In fact,

**[03:16:15.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003005)  
it has already been identified

**[03:16:17.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003006)  
ang nagiging problema po kasi,

**[03:16:21.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003007)  
Your Honor, Congressman po,

**[03:16:23.279] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003008)  
talagang because of the advances of technology,

**[03:16:26.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003009)  
talagang lahat makina na eh.

**[03:16:30.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003010)  
So, I think they're in the process now of procuring

**[03:16:35.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003011)  
or about to procure equipment that can track

**[03:16:39.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003012)  
those that are using VPNs to shadow their presence.

**[03:16:45.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003013)  
And,

**[03:16:47.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003014)  
I think that is what they will be lobbying for sa Kongreso.

**[03:16:53.279] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003015)  
So, as I mentioned earlier,

**[03:16:56.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003016)  
hindi ko po maaaring pakialaman yung kanilang teritoryo.

**[03:17:01.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003017)  
Kami po ay rekomendatory sa kanila.

**[03:17:04.239] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003018)  
And, I can say one thing.

**[03:17:06.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003019)  
The last year, our collaboration with DICT,

**[03:17:11.239] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003020)  
between DICT, CICC, NTC and PAGCOR,

**[03:17:15.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003021)  
has

**[03:17:16.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003022)  
really been so good na we continue to close so many illegal sites.

**[03:17:26.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003023)  
Unfortunately, kung anong bilis ng pagsasara,

**[03:17:28.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003024)  
ganon din bilis ang pagbubukas nila muli because of so many things that they can do.

**[03:17:36.399] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003025)  
So, that's it. That's my answer to your question, Your Honor, Kong.

**[03:17:41.960] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003026)  
Thank you. Madam Chair, if I may, just a quick anecdote about that.

**[03:17:46.160] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003027)  
I want to note the efforts of the NBI, CICC, and PAGCOR because I've actually joined them po sa mga raids nila.

**[03:17:55.439] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003028)  
And I see the effort that they're putting into it.

**[03:17:58.080] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003029)  
But the reason why I bring up this question is because could we have a list from PAGCOR

**[03:18:03.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003030)  
kung ano yung mga equipment na kailangan i-procure ng CICC at ibang law enforcement at magkano po?

**[03:18:10.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003031)  
Kasi I want to see how much it will cost versus how much revenue we would be able to,

**[03:18:15.826] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003032)  
how much revenue we would be able to generate from properly monitoring.

**[03:18:18.880] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003033)  
Kung meron po tayong projection na kung meron tayong ganitong klaseng equipment,

**[03:18:23.479] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003034)  
we will be able to increase the number of websites that we're able to monitor and take down, etc.,

**[03:18:30.239] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003035)  
how much we would be able to generate in revenue if we have proper policing through these machineries and technologies.

**[03:18:38.560] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003036)  
Madam Chair, if we make this investment in proper policing,

**[03:18:42.520] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003037)  
maybe we could increase government revenue in the succeeding year.

**[03:18:45.920] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003038)  
At least that's the way I see it.

**[03:18:47.640] Rep. Brian Poe** (asr-003039)  
Yun lang po. And thank you for the time, Madam Chair.

**[03:18:50.600] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003040)  
Thank you, Vice Chair Bryan po.

**[03:18:52.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003041)  
Actually, I think it's fitting because Vice Chair Bryan po is anyways defending the budget of the DICT.

**[03:18:57.520] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003042)  
So you can also pose that same question to the CICC when we deliberate on the budget of DICT.

**[03:19:04.399] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003043)  
But very good point.

**[03:19:06.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003044)  
Sigur po, we'll just wait, Kong Bryan, so we can also share with PAGCOR the websites,

**[03:19:12.359] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003045)  
the screenshot of the websites that you've pointed out earlier.

**[03:19:15.560] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003046)  
But it's really a matter of enforcement and reinforcement given, as Chair Al was saying,

**[03:19:21.439] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003047)  
as soon as those sites are taken down after a while, they go back up again.

**[03:19:25.399] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003048)  
So we'll bring it up.

**[03:19:29.439] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003049)  
Ikaw naman magpapreside ng DICT budget hearing.

**[03:19:33.439] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003050)  
So you can also bring it up with CICC when the time comes.

**[03:19:37.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003051)  
Thank you. Thank you, Chair Al.

**[03:19:39.319] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003052)  
Thank you so much, Vice Chair Bryan po.

**[03:19:42.160] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003053)  
We'll now move to the next interpellator.

**[03:19:45.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003054)  
From the minority, Representative Robert Nazal.

**[03:19:49.399] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003055)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[03:19:51.399] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003056)  
Good morning.

**[03:19:54.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003057)  
Good afternoon, Chairman Tengco.

**[03:20:00.120] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003058)  
Allow me first to commend PAGCOR and PAGCOR family for its commendable initiatives,

**[03:20:06.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003059)  
particularly the nationwide school building program and the donation of patient transport vehicles.

**[03:20:13.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003060)  
These efforts have brought real benefit to many of our communities and we are grateful for that.

**[03:20:20.319] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003061)  
If I may, however, raise a small concern.

**[03:20:25.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003062)  
Mayroon po ako kasing pending request na more than one year na ang anniversary

**[03:20:34.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003063)  
ng mga wheelchairs and mga ambulances para sa mga constituent natin.

**[03:20:39.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003064)  
I mentioned this not as a complaint, no?

**[03:20:42.680] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003065)  
But simply to ask in the spirit of transparency, may pagkakaiba po ba ang minority ang nag-request?

**[03:20:52.160] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003066)  
Your Honor, wala pong pagkakaiba.

**[03:20:56.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003067)  
Ang totoo po dun sa PTV, kami po nag-augment lang talaga sa PCSO.

**[03:21:02.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003068)  
At hindi naman talaga naming gawa ay magbigay.

**[03:21:05.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003069)  
Tumulong po kami. Nagpasobra na ng konti.

**[03:21:10.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003070)  
So, yung pong request niyo eh, titinan ko kaagad pagkatapos nito

**[03:21:14.319] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003071)  
ng mapatunayan sa inyong wala pong pagkakaiba ang minority at majority

**[03:21:20.479] Committee/agency brief acknowledgment** (asr-003072)  
pag ang PAGCOR po ang timitiin dito.

**[03:21:22.399] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003073)  
Thank you, thank you, Mr. Chair. Salamat po diyan.

**[03:21:25.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003074)  
So, I will go directly to my questions.

**[03:21:34.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003075)  
Madam Chair, I would like to clarify the effect of the planned privatization of Casino Filipino

**[03:21:41.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003076)  
on universal healthcare funding.

**[03:21:44.479] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003077)  
In Section 37 of the UHC Act, 50% of the national government share from PAGCOR income

**[03:21:52.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003078)  
is earmarked for PhilHealth.

**[03:21:56.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003079)  
Once PAGCOR disposes of its Casino Filipino operations and becomes purely a regulatory body,

**[03:22:04.960] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003080)  
what happens to the UHC earmark?

**[03:22:08.439] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003081)  
Will the national government share, and therefore yung 50% going to PhilHealth,

**[03:22:14.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003082)  
due to be computed from PAGCOR's entire gaming-related income,

**[03:22:19.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003083)  
including regulatory fees and license fee shares from private casinos, e-games, and other licenses?

**[03:22:28.040] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003084)  
Or will the legal revenue base covered by the UHC earmark become smaller

**[03:22:34.199] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003085)  
once PAGCOR is no longer itself a gaming operator?

**[03:22:41.279] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003086)  
Na compute na po ba ng PAGCOR with the deal,

**[03:22:44.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003087)  
together with the DOF, DBM, and PhilHealth,

**[03:22:48.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003088)  
na quantify na po ba natin yung UHC contribution before and after privatization?

**[03:22:54.399] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003089)  
And if so, can you please give the committee the projected PAGCOR contribution to UHC

**[03:23:02.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003090)  
for 2026, 2027, and 2028, and the first full year after privatization?

**[03:23:12.120] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003091)  
Thank you for the answer.

**[03:23:14.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003093)  
The question, Your Honor.

**[03:23:17.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003094)  
Yes, na compute na po, at ang totoo po eh,

**[03:23:21.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003095)  
kung meron pong nag-aaral niya ng lubos ngayon,

**[03:23:24.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003096)  
eh yung pong GCG, Governance Commission for GOCCs.

**[03:23:30.479] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003097)  
Dahil, Your Honor, Congressman Nazal,

**[03:23:35.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003098)  
ang magre-rekomenda po sa tanggapan ng Pangulo ay ang GCG.

**[03:23:42.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003099)  
Ah.

**[03:23:42.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003100)  
Sila po ang magre-rekomenda kung dapat ma-ituloy

**[03:23:47.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003101)  
or dapat matuloy ang decoupling privatization.

**[03:23:51.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003102)  
So, nakita na po namin yan, pinag-aaralan po,

**[03:23:57.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003103)  
miyembro po din ng GCG Commission, ang PAGCOR,

**[03:24:03.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003104)  
ah, ang Secretary of Finance, gayon din po ang Secretary of Budget and Management, DBM.

**[03:24:11.160] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003105)  
Sila po eh ex-officio members ito.

**[03:24:13.560] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003106)  
So, maliwanag po na pinag-aaralan po at nakita na po yan.

**[03:24:19.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003107)  
Sa aming pong internal na pag-aaral, nakita po namin na hindi po in the long run,

**[03:24:27.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003108)  
in the medium term, ah, hindi lang po magpapantay, mahigitan pa.

**[03:24:33.479] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003109)  
Because of the operational efficiency po na mangyayari.

**[03:24:38.239] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003110)  
Ah.

**[03:24:39.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003111)  
Ah.

**[03:24:39.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003112)  
Your Honor, sa ngayon po kasi, binanggit ko nga kanina eh,

**[03:24:44.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003113)  
napangakin na po ng drug ng karga ng ah, salaries and wages ng empleyado.

**[03:24:51.120] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003114)  
Gayon din naman po ng halimbawa ay yung ating ah, rentals sa different properties natin.

**[03:25:01.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003115)  
Ah, himigit ko mo lang po, one and a half billion pesos a month ang nagagastos natin dito.

**[03:25:07.276] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003116)  
Ah.

**[03:25:08.120] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003117)  
So, kapag nawala po lahat yan, eh, tataas po yung income ng PAGCOR.

**[03:25:12.880] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003118)  
And sa huli naman po, may contribution kami na minimum of 50% of our net profit.

**[03:25:19.359] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003119)  
So, that will neutralize in the long run yung pong adjustments na mangyayari.

**[03:25:25.479] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003120)  
Magkano po ba yung expected revenues natin once ma-privatize?

**[03:25:28.990] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003121)  
Ah.

**[03:25:30.080] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003122)  
And how much are you projecting will be the share on the first year or the first and second year alone?

**[03:25:36.120] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003123)  
Opo.

**[03:25:37.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003124)  
Wala po kasi, ah, Your Honor, ah, wala po kasi kaming control.

**[03:25:43.160] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003125)  
Basta yung amin lang is we remit our share to the national government.

**[03:25:49.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003126)  
And it is the national government that supposedly remits to the PhilHealth or to the Universal Healthcare Fund.

**[03:25:59.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003127)  
Ang liniliwanag ko po kanina pa, we have been updated.

**[03:26:04.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003128)  
Updated po tayo sa remittance.

**[03:26:06.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003129)  
So, nasa sa national treasury na po yung pagbibigay.

**[03:26:11.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003130)  
Kasi meron pong kumisang hindi pagkakaintindihan na kami po ang nagre-remit sa Universal Healthcare Fund.

**[03:26:19.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003131)  
Ang nangyayari po, on a monthly basis, kami po ay nagbibigay sa national treasury.

**[03:26:30.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003132)  
At pagkatapos, obligasyon na rin po at responsibilidad nila ang mag-remit sa Universal Healthcare Fund.

**[03:26:40.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003133)  
So, sa ngayon po, I just want to clarify this, just like I, how I answered earlier, I think you were not present.

**[03:26:47.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003134)  
Wala po kaming control dun sa remittance to the, because ang batas po maliwanag.

**[03:26:54.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003135)  
Remit your share to the national government and the national government will remit 50% of what you have remitted to the Universal Healthcare Fund.

**[03:27:06.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003136)  
So, what you're saying, Mr. Chair, Madam Chair, is that you have no figures on how much is being remitted to PhilHealth?

**[03:27:15.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003137)  
Well, we, it's not that we do not have figures, we do not have control.

**[03:27:19.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003138)  
But, I'll just give you an example, for year 2025, for example, we remitted more or less 50 billion pesos to the national treasury.

**[03:27:31.279] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003139)  
That means, following the intent of the law.

**[03:27:34.190] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003140)  
Dapat.

**[03:27:34.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003141)  
25 billion.

**[03:27:38.570] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003142)  
Yes.

**[03:27:42.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003143)  
But, the reason you're saying this, Madam Chair, is because, meron po bang naging deviation from the, what you remitted to the national treasury?

**[03:27:50.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003144)  
Wala ah, hindi na po namin alam yun eh.

**[03:27:53.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003145)  
Sapagkat sabi ko nga, buwanan po, we remit to the national treasury.

**[03:27:57.460] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003146)  
Yeah.

**[03:27:58.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003147)  
And, the law states, the national treasury or the national government should remit,

**[03:28:06.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003148)  
should remit 50% of what we have remitted to the Universal Healthcare Fund.

**[03:28:12.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003149)  
Now, whether they're remitting or not is beyond our control already.

**[03:28:16.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003150)  
That is what I'm trying to point out, Your Honor.

**[03:28:20.560] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003151)  
Ah, and that was asked earlier also.

**[03:28:22.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003152)  
So, kasi may akala po sila na diretsyo po kaming nagre-remit sa Universal Healthcare Fund.

**[03:28:29.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003153)  
Hindi po ganon.

**[03:28:31.120] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003154)  
Ang batas po ay maliwanag.

**[03:28:32.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003155)  
Remit 50% of your revenue to the national government and the government under the Universal Healthcare Act must remit 50% of what we have remitted to the Universal Healthcare Fund.

**[03:28:48.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003156)  
Considering the privatization, ah, Madam Chair, meron po bang, meron po ba kayo nakikita na dapat baguhin ng Congress in terms of legislation para, ah, mag-guarantee na ganon pa rin yung magiging,

**[03:29:02.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003157)  
income or share ng UHC sa kikitain ng, ah, sa manggagaling po sa privatization?

**[03:29:10.279] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003158)  
Opo, ah, kami po ay, ah, gumawa na rin ng aming mga sariling computations.

**[03:29:17.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003159)  
I think, ah, may, may certain numbers kami.

**[03:29:21.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003160)  
Pero this we can share with the committee.

**[03:29:25.279] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003161)  
Maybe the committee can share with you.

**[03:29:27.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003162)  
But, ah, we will forward what we have done internally.

**[03:29:31.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003163)  
But making clear that sa ngayon po, yun ni base lamang sa projections namin after the implementation of the decoupling slash privatization.

**[03:29:45.825] Committee/agency brief acknowledgment** (asr-003164)  
Your Honor.

**[03:29:50.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003165)  
Thank you for that, Madam Chair, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:29:58.640] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003166)  
Kung magkakaroon po tayo ng transitional revenue habang naglilipat ng operations sa private sector, ang PAGCOR,

**[03:30:09.279] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003167)  
ang one-time windfall mula sa privatization ay dapat magsilbing safety cushion para sa UHC.

**[03:30:17.520] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003168)  
Since PAGCOR estimates P30 to P50 billion in proceeds from the sale of casino Filipino assets and licenses,

**[03:30:24.720] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003169)  
direct UHC collections might experience transition friction.

**[03:30:30.840] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003170)  
So, is the Executive willing to commit or does Congress need to legislate a sort of transitional healthcare stabilization fund

**[03:30:39.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003171)  
sourced directly from the privatization proceeds to guarantee that PhilHealth's annual subsidy is protected?

**[03:30:46.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003172)  
Para captured pa rin po yung windfall?

**[03:30:50.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003173)  
Your Honor, we can look into that.

**[03:30:54.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003174)  
As I said earlier in another question, the priority use of the proceeds of the retirement,

**[03:31:02.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003175)  
which is now being trimmed down to about 30 on the high side because of the decline,

**[03:31:09.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003176)  
our priority is being able to pay the retirement of our employees because that, for me,

**[03:31:16.160] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003177)  
is a commitment to protect the interests of our PAGCOR employees.

**[03:31:22.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003178)  
So, coming from kung magkakaroon po ng difference doon sa proceeds and yung binayad natin sa retirement,

**[03:31:32.479] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003179)  
then I think that can be considered as a possibility na magkaroon ng cushion fund, as you had mentioned earlier.

**[03:31:44.120] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003180)  
But I wish to state...

**[03:31:46.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003181)  
that from my standpoint, the priority also for me is being able to pay the retirement benefits

**[03:31:58.560] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003182)  
and the special incentive package of those who will be affected by this move to decouple PAGCOR.

**[03:32:09.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003183)  
Magkano po ba yung ano? How much will we spend?

**[03:32:14.439] Committee/agency brief acknowledgment** (asr-003184)  
To ano?

**[03:32:16.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003185)  
Retirement.

**[03:32:16.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003186)  
Your Honor, you're talking for retirement.

**[03:32:18.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003187)  
Yes.

**[03:32:19.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003188)  
We're looking at about 28 to 30 billion as the money needed for the retirement.

**[03:32:36.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003189)  
Today, we have close to about 12 billion.

**[03:32:42.279] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003190)  
So, we will be subsidizing coming from the sale.

**[03:32:45.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003191)  
And the difference, as I mentioned, maybe that can be...

**[03:32:49.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003193)  
Thank you for your comments.

**[03:32:52.479] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003194)  
And we can study whether that difference can be used as a stabilization fund for the universal healthcare.

**[03:33:02.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003195)  
How much, Madam Chair, is the expected windfall?

**[03:33:08.279] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003196)  
You're talking from where, Your Honor?

**[03:33:10.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003197)  
From the privatization of...

**[03:33:12.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003198)  
We're looking at about mga 25 to 30 billion po sa pangkasalukuyan.

**[03:33:19.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003199)  
And as I said...

**[03:33:21.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003200)  
As I said, we have a current retirement fund of about 12 billion.

**[03:33:27.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003201)  
So, magkakaroon po ng positive later on, I'm sure, kahit papano.

**[03:33:33.239] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003202)  
Because existing...

**[03:33:35.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003203)  
Currently po, ang retirement fund ay nagkakahalaga ng mga 12 billion pesos cash in bank na po yun.

**[03:33:42.439] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003204)  
But after all the expenses, all the retirement benefits and whatever you need to pay,

**[03:33:51.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003205)  
upon completion of the privatization, saan po initially dapat ilalagay yung windfall?

**[03:33:57.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003206)  
Normally po yun.

**[03:33:58.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003207)  
Iri-remit?

**[03:34:00.160] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003208)  
That becomes extraordinary income.

**[03:34:03.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003209)  
And that being so, we will be remitting 50% of that later on to the National Treasury.

**[03:34:11.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003210)  
Sana nga po, doon na lang sa kusyon ilagay and i-prioritize natin.

**[03:34:15.601] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003211)  
Well, I think, again...

**[03:34:16.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003212)  
So that the UHC is prioritized, hindi po maapektuhan.

**[03:34:20.160] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003216)  
I'd like to thank you for your suggestion so that we can look into the matter, Your Honor.

**[03:34:26.800] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003217)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:34:27.760] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003218)  
Thank you to the PAGCOR family.

**[03:34:29.439] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003219)  
And please expect your request to be delivered within 48 hours.

**[03:34:34.359] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003220)  
Thank you, sir.

**[03:34:36.920] Rep. Roberto Nazal** (asr-003221)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[03:34:39.279] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003222)  
Thank you very much, Representative Nazal.

**[03:34:41.800] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003223)  
As committed by Chair Al, like all the documentary requirements would be provided within 48 hours after the hearing.

**[03:34:49.479] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003224)  
We will do.

**[03:34:50.601] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003225)

So, the earnings of PAGCOR come from casino funds.

**[03:47:44.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003323)  
Filipino operations, licensed casinos, digital gaming, e-games, and non-gaming income.

Now, when it comes to casino Filipino operations, because the data that I have is past 2024,

**[03:48:01.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003325)

38?

38?

**[03:48:41.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003334)

**[03:49:15.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003341)  
Php 31.5. So it's also down from Php 33 in 2024.

**[03:49:22.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003343)

**[03:49:34.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003345)

**[03:49:47.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003347)

**[03:49:52.760] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003348)  
Okay. Now, for digital e-games, how much is the projected revenue for 2026?

**[03:49:59.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003349)  
The projected revenue for 2026 is Php 38 billion.

**[03:50:04.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003350)  
Php 38 to 40 billion.

**[03:50:06.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003351)  
Php 38 to 40 billion.

**[03:50:08.279] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003352)

**[03:50:15.132] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003353)  
Although as I have already mentioned earlier, now the trend is that licensed casinos are gradually surpassing online gaming.

**[03:50:27.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003354)

**[03:50:42.439] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003357)

**[03:50:49.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003357-split-2)  
Plus in the sense na kung wala po ito, lalong hirap na hirap na ang PAGCOR sa pangkasalukuyan dahil pataas po yung gastos at napakalaki ng sweldo ng mga empleyado. So kung hindi po dumating yung online gaming, malaki na po ang problema ng PAGCOR sa pangkasalukuyan.

**[03:51:10.803] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003358)  
If online gaming does not come, the problem of PAGCOR in the present is already big.

**[03:51:17.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003358-split-2)  
But a minus because I'm concerned that the big licensees are also seeing a decline in revenue.

**[03:51:37.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003359)  
It's just one full circle.

**[03:51:41.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003360)  
There's a time when it's really low, there's a time when it's going to rise again.

**[03:51:45.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003361)  
Like what we all saw, when we had a problem with COVID, when we had the pandemic,

**[03:51:57.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003362)

**[03:52:00.120] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003363)

**[03:52:06.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003364)

**[03:52:12.239] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003365)  
Now, because of all these problems we are encountering,

**[03:52:15.479] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003366)

**[03:52:22.228] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003367)

**[03:52:31.088] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003368)

**[03:52:35.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003369)

**[03:52:39.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003370)

**[03:52:43.861] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003371)

**[03:52:51.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003372)

**[03:52:55.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003373)

**[03:53:15.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003377)

**[03:53:21.759] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003378)

**[03:53:26.447] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003379)
