**[03:53:27.856] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003380)  
I have a question.

**[03:53:29.120] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003381)  
Because while the concern of others,

**[03:53:32.159] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003382)  
while their concern is the dwindling revenue,

**[03:53:35.327] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003383)  
the dwindling revenue of PAGCOR,

**[03:53:37.319] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003384)  
my concern is different.

**[03:53:39.560] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003385)  
Where will the revenue of PAGCOR come from?

**[03:53:44.040] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003386)  
Because, as you mentioned earlier,

**[03:53:48.239] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003387)  
online gaming is increasing.

**[03:53:50.013] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003388)  
You also mentioned earlier, Chairman Al,

**[03:53:53.039] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003389)  
that usually, the one who plays online games comes from CDE.

**[03:53:58.600] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003390)  
So, it means, that's where the money will come from.

**[03:54:02.399] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003391)  
And then we are spending, well, you are giving,

**[03:54:06.279] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003393)  
what?

**[03:54:07.279] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003394)  
How much did you give for the share for the social programs of the President?

**[03:54:16.120] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003395)  
And also, the one you are using for social outreach?

**[03:54:21.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003396)  
How many percent?

**[03:54:23.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003397)  
How many billion?

**[03:54:25.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003398)  
A billion for social outreach.

**[03:54:29.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003399)  
Maybe in one year, there will be a decrease of about 15 million.

**[03:54:35.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003401)  
15 to 18 billion.

**[03:54:36.680] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003402)  
15 to 18 billion.

**[03:54:38.760] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003403)  
So, isn't it ironic that the one who is in charge of online gaming

**[03:54:44.927] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003404)  
is getting money from CDE,

**[03:54:47.736] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003405)  
and then we will release money for social projects

**[03:54:53.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003406)  
in the amount of about 18 billion,

**[03:54:55.760] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003407)  
not to mention the President's Social Fund also,

**[03:55:00.093] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003408)  
which is also being used there,

**[03:55:02.199] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003409)  
also to help the CDE.

**[03:55:04.055] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003410)  
The people who are also getting money,

**[03:55:08.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003411)  
the same class of people,

**[03:55:09.540] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003412)  
where the money is also coming from.

**[03:55:12.439] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003413)  
That's why, that's the cause of my concern.

**[03:55:15.840] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003414)  
Because fundamentally, the architectural framework of online gaming is different.

**[03:55:24.800] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003415)  
Unlike physical casinos, there are also physical barriers.

**[03:55:31.199] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003416)  
Before you can enter,

**[03:55:33.079] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003417)  
you will see if you are young, if you are poor, if you have money,

**[03:55:38.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003418)  
unlike online gaming.

**[03:55:42.199] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003419)  
In fact, this was the same question that I asked PAGCOR during the 18th Congress,

**[03:55:46.894] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003420)  
when they allowed E-Sabong.

**[03:55:49.439] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003421)  
I could not understand why,

**[03:55:51.199] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003422)  
during the time when the economy was closed,

**[03:55:55.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003423)  
there was a lockdown,

**[03:55:57.260] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003424)  
the Filipinos were unable to work,

**[03:56:01.479] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003425)  
the government allowed the program,

**[03:56:03.721] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003426)  
the government allowed the proliferation of E-Sabong.

**[03:56:09.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003427)  
The security guards, the children, the people without money.

**[03:56:14.239] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003428)  
So, isn't it important that before we open or expand online gaming,

**[03:56:23.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003429)  
we have to strengthen first our regulatory powers

**[03:56:27.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003430)  
on how to police

**[03:56:30.653] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003431)  
those who are playing games?

**[03:56:36.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003432)  
Your Honor, I totally agree with you,

**[03:56:39.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003433)  
with your comments.

**[03:56:41.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003434)  
But unfortunately,

**[03:56:45.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003435)  
advances in technology are too fast

**[03:56:48.346] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003436)  
that we cannot not follow suit.

**[03:56:50.657] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003437)  
Because what will happen, Your Honor,

**[03:56:53.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003438)  
if we don't follow suit,

**[03:56:57.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003439)  
it will be illegal for the technology to flourish.

**[03:57:00.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003440)  
Because the illegals are the ones who are saddened.

**[03:57:03.917] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003441)  
There are no illegals here in the Philippines.

**[03:57:05.305] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003442)  
We are being targeted outside.

**[03:57:07.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003443)  
So, I understand what you are saying.

**[03:57:15.114] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003444)  
But we cannot continue to fight for the present.

**[03:57:20.991] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003445)  
Because we will eat the illegals.

**[03:57:24.157] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003446)  
The illegals are continuing.

**[03:57:25.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003447)  
That's why Congressman Ryan mentioned earlier that the place we closed that he requested is now open again.

**[03:57:33.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003448)  
So, no matter how hard it is, we need to look at what you are saying.

**[03:57:42.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003449)  
At the same time, we need to do the same with our online gaming.

**[03:57:50.254] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003450)  
Because if not, we will eat our lives.

**[03:57:54.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003451)  
So, I totally agree with you, Your Honor.

**[03:57:59.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003452)  
We are doing whatever we can.

**[03:58:01.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003453)  
We are strengthening our structures and our online gaming guidelines, rules and regulations.

**[03:58:10.479] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003454)  
We are updating it on a regular basis based on market trends.

**[03:58:16.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003455)  
But unfortunately, we also have to...

**[03:58:22.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003456)  
go head-to-head with the online gaming industry.

**[03:58:28.338] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003457)  
Because if we do not follow this, we will not be able to catch up with the illegal operators

**[03:58:36.321] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003458)  
who are constantly harassing our fellow citizens.

**[03:58:41.600] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003459)  
Yes, Chairman Alf.

**[03:58:42.640] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003460)  
I totally agree with you.

**[03:58:44.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003461)  
In fact, I'm not against online gaming.

**[03:58:46.439] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003462)  
What I'm saying is...

**[03:58:48.279] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003463)  
What I'm against is...

**[03:58:51.399] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003465)  
How can we properly police the people who play online games?

**[03:59:00.479] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003466)  
How do we police them?

**[03:59:02.520] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003467)  
Whether they're minors, whether they come from the...

**[03:59:06.080] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003468)  
That's what you're saying.

**[03:59:07.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003469)  
We know that it comes from CDE.

**[03:59:10.560] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003470)  
And to allow them to continue to gamble...

**[03:59:15.373] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003471)  
There's always a complaint.

**[03:59:17.973] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003472)  
There's a shortage of medicine to buy.

**[03:59:19.487] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003473)  
There's a shortage of food to buy.

**[03:59:21.227] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003474)  
There's a shortage of electricity to pay.

**[03:59:23.160] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003475)  
There's a shortage of education for the children.

**[03:59:25.960] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003476)  
But usually, as your data would show...

**[03:59:32.600] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003477)  
They are usually the customers of online gaming sites.

**[03:59:37.880] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003478)  
And I think...

**[03:59:40.840] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003479)  
We have to concentrate first on how to properly police these people.

**[03:59:45.720] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003480)  
Otherwise...

**[03:59:47.120] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003482)  
Kawawa naman yung walang mangyayari sa bansa natin.

**[03:59:51.760] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003483)  
In fact, the reason why, for example, in China, why they have restricted gambling,

**[03:59:58.560] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003484)  
kasi ayaw nilang nagsusugal yung mga insek, masusto nilang magtrabaho yung mga kanilang mga citizens.

**[04:00:11.600] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003485)  
And dito sa atin nga, what are the concrete steps that we have tried to take?

**[04:00:19.600] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003486)  
I understand that technology is evolving very fast, but we're not doing anything.

**[04:00:29.120] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003487)  
Walang pinagkaiba yan. Naalala ko nung tinesting ng gobyerno yung online na motorcycle na sinabi nila,

**[04:00:40.399] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003488)  
hindi, kayaan muna.

**[04:00:41.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003489)  
Kayaan natin sila mag-operate without having a regulatory framework.

**[04:00:45.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003490)  
By the time na ang dami nang nandun sa platform, hindi na nila kayang i-regulate.

**[04:00:51.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003491)  
Baka ganyan din yung mangyayari sa atin.

**[04:00:57.920] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003492)  
I can only answer that I guess we just have to look into it and see how we can strengthen the structures at the same time.

**[04:01:08.199] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003493)  
Well, yun lang Madam Chair. I hope we really look into it and come up with ways to,

**[04:01:14.120] Rep. Jesus "Bong" C. Suntay** (asr-003494)  
to properly regulate it bago, bago ano, bago dumating sa size na hindi na natin kayang kontrolin talaga.

**[04:01:23.760] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003495)  
Thank you very much, Chair.

**[04:01:25.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003496)  
Thank you.

**[04:01:26.840] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003497)  
Thank you, Representative Bong Sunday.

**[04:01:29.399] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003498)  
Our next interpellator, Representative Mica Gonzales.

**[04:01:34.239] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003499)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:01:36.399] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003500)  
So I think, Chairman Al, I think you feel the sentiment of our colleagues is to protect

**[04:01:43.359] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003501)  
the Filipino people from excessive gambling, di ba po?

**[04:01:47.560] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003502)  
So in relating to that, I would like to ask, kasi ba, from the previous committee hearings of the Ames and Amusement here in the House,

**[04:01:56.399] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003503)  
I asked regarding setting a cap or setting a limit per user.

**[04:02:02.760] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003504)  
For example, meron tayong online gaming and meron tayong user na player.

**[04:02:09.640] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003505)  
I asked them previously kung sino ba nasa set.

**[04:02:13.159] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003506)  
Nang cap or nang maximum bet nila per day or I don't know how we should call it, no?

**[04:02:20.880] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003507)  
Wala pala tayong sineset na cap per player.

**[04:02:25.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003508)  
So I would like to ask if up until now po, wala pa rin po ba kayong sineset na cap or limit per player in a day or in a week?

**[04:02:37.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003509)  
Sa ngayon po, wala.

**[04:02:39.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003510)  
I'd be very candid and honest about it.

**[04:02:42.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003511)  
Your Honor, we have not set, but I believe in the online gaming law that is being proposed,

**[04:02:55.120] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003512)  
there will be an inclusion of these caps, whether caps on deposits and caps of gaming activity.

**[04:03:04.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003513)  
Yes, because...

**[04:03:06.080] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003514)  
Yun po ang, Your Honor, yun po ang gustong patunguan.

**[04:03:13.359] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003515)  
Nang parehong committee sa Senado, gayon din sa Kongreso, para po ma-address yung binabanggit ninyo.

**[04:03:19.880] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003516)  
Your Honor.

**[04:03:22.120] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003517)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:03:23.479] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003518)  
Next question po.

**[04:03:26.479] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003519)  
Since it was also opened na yung online gaming is mas madali, is more accessible compared to, you know, going to a casino, you know, playing there.

**[04:03:36.239] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003520)  
And we all know that government employees are not allowed to play sa casino.

**[04:03:43.120] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003521)  
And do we have, sa online gaming po ba, how can they detect na, oh, ito, ano to, polis to, oh, ito, they work for the government.

**[04:03:52.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003522)  
Mayroon po ba tayong mechanism to ensure that government employees will not and are not allowed to play, even if it's online gaming?

**[04:04:03.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003523)  
Your Honor, definitely, we are strictly implementing the KYC process,

**[04:04:11.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003524)  
meaning, know your customer.

**[04:04:14.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003525)  
Lahat po ng licenses ay dapat suriin ng mabuti yung mga magre-rehistro sa kanila.

**[04:04:23.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003526)  
At kung dumating po yung pagkakataon na nakalusot sila, yung secondary screening po naman na ginagawa ng PAGCOR,

**[04:04:32.239] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003527)  
and we have been very successful, Your Honor, ay yung pagvo-void ng kanilang panalo.

**[04:04:40.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003528)  
So, kung sa kanilang...

**[04:04:42.178] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003529)  
Kung sa kanilang may nakalusot na isang government employee na nakapaglaro at sila'y swinerte at nanalo,

**[04:04:52.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003530)  
bago po nila ma-withdraw yun, marami din pong screening yun.

**[04:04:56.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003531)  
So, pag na-discovery po na sila'y empleyado ng gobyerno, whether siya po'y isang opisyal o isang karaniwang empleyado lamang,

**[04:05:08.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003532)  
we void the winnings.

**[04:05:11.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003533)  
And I can say, I can tell you and members of the committee that in the last two years,

**[04:05:20.479] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003534)  
we have voided close to half a billion pesos worth of winnings already sapagkat na-discovery po namin

**[04:05:28.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003535)  
na ang mga nanalong ito ay mga empleyado o kawani ng ating pamahalaan.

**[04:05:35.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003536)  
Yun po yung secondary screening process.

**[04:05:40.560] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003537)  
Natupad po namin nitong panunungkulan ko bilang Pagcourt Chairman and CEO.

**[04:05:46.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003538)  
Pero ang mahalaga po at ang unang hakbang ay sa registration pa lamang, yung pong tinataw na KYC.

**[04:05:56.239] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003539)  
Unfortunately, Your Honor, meron pa pong nakalulusot doon.

**[04:06:01.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003540)  
Nagsusumiti ng fake na ID halimbawa or hindi dinidisclose na sila'y kawani ng pamahalaan,

**[04:06:08.560] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003541)  
eh meron.

**[04:06:10.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003542)  
Meron pa rin pong gano'n.

**[04:06:11.560] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003543)  
At sabi ko nga, doon pong pagsala roon, eh yun ang pag mag-reclaim na sila ng premyo,

**[04:06:19.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003544)  
doon naman papasok yung paghihingi muli ng final submission mo ng ID.

**[04:06:27.840] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003545)  
At doon nga po, matagumpay namin masasabi na naging maganda yung programa

**[04:06:34.840] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003546)  
dahil sa marami na po kaming vinoid na winnings.

**[04:06:39.640] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003547)  
Yun lang.

**[04:06:40.359] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003548)  
Thank you, Your Honor.

**[04:06:41.359] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003549)  
Thank you, Madam Chair Chairman Al.

**[04:06:44.239] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003550)  
That is for the kasino po na pwedeng puntahan but I would also like to ask if meron na po ba tayong sort of security mechanism sa online gaming?

**[04:06:54.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003551)  
Like yung mga, alam mo yung sa Facebook, yung mga ads.

**[04:06:57.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003552)  
So do we have some sort of mechanism to ensure that the player is not a government employee?

**[04:07:03.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003553)  
Yes, that is through the KYC process, ma'am.

**[04:07:07.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003554)  
Ah.

**[04:07:08.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003555)  
Yung iba po yung KYC.

**[04:07:13.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003556)  
Ang totoo, maski magsubok kayo, hindi kayo makakamagiging member or magiging player ng isang legal license kasino

**[04:07:25.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003557)  
kung ikaw ay government employee dahilan sa KYC.

**[04:07:30.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003558)  
Ang malaki pong problema, Your Honor, yung iligal po.

**[04:07:35.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003559)  
Sapagkat sa iligal, wala pong KYC process.

**[04:07:38.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003560)  
Maging sa edad?

**[04:07:40.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003561)  
Occupation or identification, yun po ang malaking problema natin.

**[04:07:46.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003562)  
Kaya, Your Honor, po ulit-ulit kong sinasabi, ang problema po ng industriyang ito ay yung iligal.

**[04:07:56.119] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003563)  
Dahil sa iligal operators, tinatanggap po maski minor de edad, maski teenager.

**[04:08:03.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003564)  
Samantalang dito po sa proseso natin sa may lisensya sa online,

**[04:08:09.279] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003565)  
halos, sabihin na natin, high 90s in percentage na ang dumataan sa very strict process of KYC.

**[04:08:22.560] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003566)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:08:23.600] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003567)  
So, last question na lamang po.

**[04:08:26.399] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003568)  
Since we have stern warning sa mga kasino operators po,

**[04:08:34.199] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003569)  
do we also have sort of mechanism of,

**[04:08:38.040] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003570)  
mechanism on players na they could be permanently blocked from a casino?

**[04:08:42.960] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003571)  
Like for example, I don't know, do we have some sort of instances na we permanently blocked off a player in entering a casino?

**[04:08:53.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003572)  
Your Honor, I did report earlier, as of today, we have a list that contains 15,000 names,

**[04:09:05.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003573)  
kung saan, they are not allowed to enter any of the casinos,

**[04:09:10.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003574)  
nor they are allowed to play in online gaming platforms.

**[04:09:15.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003575)  
Yun po ay na-generate natin sa pamamagitan ng programang self-exclusion at family exclusion.

**[04:09:24.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003576)  
Kung saan, ang isang nalululun na sa sugal ay pwedeng sumulat sa akin or sa PAGCOR,

**[04:09:33.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003577)  
para magsabing ayoko na magsugal, ipaban niyo po ako.

**[04:09:37.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003578)  
So, that ban is there for years.

**[04:09:42.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003579)  
Hindi po natin linilift basta-basta yun.

**[04:09:44.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003580)  
Pagdating naman po sa, kung ikaw ay nakalista sa National Database of Restricted Personnel,

**[04:09:53.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003581)  
yun po ay nakakalat din sa lahat ng kasinos at marami po ang nahuhuli

**[04:10:00.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003582)  
na hindi pwedeng makapaglaro.

**[04:10:05.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003583)  
Meron din po kaming family exclusion program kung saan ang isang asawa or ang kapamilya

**[04:10:12.479] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003584)  
ay pwedeng mag-request sa PAGCOR na hindi na pwedeng papasukin ang kanyang kamag-anak,

**[04:10:21.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003585)  
kanyang asawa, ang kanyang ama, ang kanyang anak sa mga casinos at online gaming sites natin.

**[04:10:30.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003586)  
15,000 na po ang numero na meron tayo.

**[04:10:38.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003587)  
At nitong nakaraang taon lamang, meron pong halos labing isang libo mahigit

**[04:10:45.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003588)  
ang nag-apply for self-exclusion program ng PAGCOR.

**[04:10:53.080] Rep. Alyssa Michaela "Mica" M. Gonzales** (asr-003589)  
Okay, thank you. That will be all. Thank you Chairman Al and thank you Madam Chair.

**[04:10:57.040] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003590)  
Salamat din po, Your Honor.

**[04:10:59.239] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003591)  
Thank you, Representative Mica Gonzales.

**[04:11:03.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003592)  
There's one more signed up from the minority, Representative Jernie Jett Nisay.

**[04:11:09.000] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003593)  
However, as I'm not seeing Kong Jet here, we'll move to the majority.

**[04:11:14.680] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003594)  
Our next interpellator, Representative Shirlyn Bañas-Nograles.

**[04:11:18.840] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003595)  
Thank you, Madam Chair.

**[04:11:20.800] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003596)  
Since it's past lunch time already, I'll try to be brief.

**[04:11:25.520] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003597)  
I just have two queries.

**[04:11:27.560] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003598)  
Still on online gambling.

**[04:11:30.760] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003599)  
Because we've noticed, just a follow-through po sa earlier queries.

**[04:11:34.720] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003600)  
Since we've noticed that it's becoming prevalent to the detriment of the vulnerable sectors.

**[04:11:42.119] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003601)  
So, and given po the easy access to mobile portals via payment gateways,

**[04:11:52.080] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003602)  
meron po ba tayong stringent technical protocol imposed on licenses?

**[04:12:00.279] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003603)  
To prevent the minors, government employees, and even the low-income earners from gambling.

**[04:12:07.520] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003604)  
Kasi especially po yung micro-bets, it allows one peso to five pesos.

**[04:12:12.239] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003605)  
So, even yung mga four-piece beneficiaries natin, talagang marami na rin po ang nalululong.

**[04:12:18.640] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003606)  
At second question ko po, isahin ko na lang para mabilis.

**[04:12:22.880] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003607)  
In the report po, your relief operations for calamity victims,

**[04:12:27.800] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003608)  
I'm just interested to know how you allocate and operationalize this po.

**[04:12:34.040] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003609)  
If I may be allowed, I want to be a bit specific.

**[04:12:37.600] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003610)  
In the recent earthquake that happened in General Santos City,

**[04:12:42.080] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003611)  
maybe some details just eluded me.

**[04:12:44.960] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003612)  
Meron po bang assistance extended yung pub core to General Santos City?

**[04:12:49.840] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003613)  
And second po, construction of classrooms

**[04:12:56.159] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003614)  
is also included among the priority projects of pub core.

**[04:13:03.040] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003615)  
In Jansan, we have like 1,400 classrooms that were badly damaged by the 7.8 earthquake.

**[04:13:10.279] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003616)  
May we know po if there has been commitment also from pub core,

**[04:13:13.680] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003617)  
since it is mentioned among your priority program.

**[04:13:17.479] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003618)  
Your Honor, ma'am.

**[04:13:20.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003619)  
You're asking what's the process?

**[04:13:22.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003620)  
The process is when there's a major calamity,

**[04:13:26.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003621)  
Doon po muna tayo sa first question.

**[04:13:28.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003622)  
Is there a stringent technical protocol?

**[04:13:31.119] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003623)  
The stringent measure, Your Honor, is the KYC process.

**[04:13:36.119] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003624)  
Know your customer process.

**[04:13:38.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003625)  
You cannot register in an online gaming,

**[04:13:42.640] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003626)  
I'm sure you're referring to online gaming.

**[04:13:45.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003627)  
It will be difficult for you to register in an online gaming app

**[04:13:49.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003628)  
to become a regular customer or player

**[04:13:53.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003629)  
if you do not go to a,

**[04:13:55.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003630)  
KYC.

**[04:13:57.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003631)  
And in that KYC, as I was explaining to Kong Micah earlier,

**[04:14:05.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003632)  
bago po kayo makapaglaro, dapat makumpleto niyo yun.

**[04:14:08.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003633)  
And doon po ay nandun lahat ng datos ninyo.

**[04:14:14.560] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003634)  
Whether you are a government employee,

**[04:14:17.080] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003635)  
whether, because you will have to present a valid government ID in that process.

**[04:14:25.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003636)  
So,

**[04:14:25.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003637)  
that is being implemented.

**[04:14:27.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003638)  
Ang sinasabi ko po earlier,

**[04:14:30.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003639)  
Your Honor, ang problema po natin,

**[04:14:32.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003640)  
kaya maraming nalululun talaga,

**[04:14:35.840] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003641)  
ay yung mga illegal operators na walang lisensya sa PAGCOR.

**[04:14:40.239] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003642)  
They compose 50% of the total industry.

**[04:14:45.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003643)  
Doon po kasi,

**[04:14:47.239] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003644)  
Kasi wala ni isang uri ng proseso.

**[04:14:50.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003645)  
Kaya kabataan ka, kaya mahirap ka, kaya nagtatrabaho ka sa pamahalan, pwede kang maglaro doon.

**[04:14:58.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003646)  
Yun po ang nakasisira sa lehiti mong mga may lisensya.

**[04:15:04.199] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003647)  
So that is my answer to question number one.

**[04:15:08.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003648)  
With regard to your question number two, yes, we did support the victims of General Santos

**[04:15:17.399] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003649)  
by giving financial and relief packages.

**[04:15:27.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003650)  
And we coordinated this with the Office of Civil Defense.

**[04:15:31.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003651)  
Sila po ang katulong namin nag-airlift ng lahat ng support at tulong namin.

**[04:15:37.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003652)  
Now,

**[04:15:38.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003653)  
on your question number two,

**[04:15:42.880] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003654)  
aside from that, you were asking what is the process?

**[04:15:46.800] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003655)  
Pag may malaki pong kalamigat,

**[04:15:50.680] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003656)  
we already voluntarily help.

**[04:15:56.239] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003657)  
Hindi na po humingi.

**[04:15:57.199] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003658)  
Pero, kung meron po kayo sa lugar ninyo na malaking disaster, malaking problema,

**[04:16:03.840] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003659)  
all you have to do is write me a letter or write PAGCOR a letter.

**[04:16:08.000] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003660)  
And wala pong problema yun, we support.

**[04:16:10.880] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003661)  
In fact, in the last week and a half, we have sent out more than 100,000 relief packs all over the Philippines.

**[04:16:21.920] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003662)  
And some of your colleagues have received also help.

**[04:16:27.199] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003663)  
So, all you have to do, ma'am, is just write us and we will respond to the best of our ability.

**[04:16:34.640] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003664)  
Thank you, sir.

**[04:16:35.520] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003665)  
Maybe you can help us also with the classroom.

**[04:16:37.840] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003666)  
Classroom constructions because lots of our classrooms are really badly damaged because of...

**[04:16:42.800] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003667)  
Yung pulang kasing reconstruction, we are not allowed sa repair.

**[04:16:48.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003668)  
Rehabilitation.

**[04:16:49.119] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003669)  
O, rehabilitation.

**[04:16:49.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003670)  
Kaya po, itong programa namin ito, the construction of 125 new school buildings

**[04:16:58.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003671)  
having an equivalent of about 1,200 classrooms na bago.

**[04:17:04.840] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003672)  
Okay, sir.

**[04:17:05.960] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003673)  
I will...

**[04:17:06.600] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003674)  
We will surely write...

**[04:17:07.920] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003675)  
I'll communicate with PAGCOR.

**[04:17:09.319] Rep. Shirlyn L. Bañas-Nograles** (asr-003676)  
Thank you very much, po.

**[04:17:10.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003677)  
Salamat din po sa inyo, Your Honor.

**[04:17:13.920] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003678)  
Thank you very much, Representative Banias Nograles.

**[04:17:17.359] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003679)  
As there are no other interpellators from the minority, we'll go to another member from the majority,

**[04:17:23.080] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003680)  
Representative Tony Roman.

**[04:17:25.279] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003681)  
Magandang tanghali po sa inyo.

**[04:17:26.920] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003682)  
Sana nakakain kayo habang sumasagot sa mga tanong.

**[04:17:32.439] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003683)  
Ito kong tinitin ako itong Comparting Results of Operations.

**[04:17:37.600] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003684)  
Note that there was a decline from 2025 through 2027 projected for the income.

**[04:17:44.359] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003685)  
And also do note that having attended the session earlier, that you have been faithfully remitting 50% sa pamalaan.

**[04:17:56.119] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003686)  
Meron itong item dito, yung Social Civic Project. Ano po yun? Ito po yung CSR?

**[04:18:00.680] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003687)  
O, ito po yung CSR projects.

**[04:18:04.960] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003688)  
Kasama na po rito yung...

**[04:18:07.640] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003689)  
Yung pagpapatayo po ng 125...

**[04:18:12.319] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003690)  
Madam Chair, can you put 20 seconds, please? I lost 20 seconds.

**[04:18:18.880] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003691)  
Sorry po. Sorry to cut you off.

**[04:18:20.239] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003692)  
Pero nung the time is supposed to stop when I stop asking questions.

**[04:18:24.479] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003693)  
Pero tumutuloy, nakikita ko gumagana.

**[04:18:27.199] Rep. Mikaela Angela B. Suansing** (asr-003694)  
Pasensya na po. Tuloy niyo lang.

**[04:18:29.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003695)  
Your Honor, yun po yung Social Civic Projects namin

**[04:18:36.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003696)  
that PAGCOR gives to...

**[04:18:39.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003697)  
the different localities all over the Philippines.

**[04:18:44.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003698)  
Kasama na rin po diyan, kaya lumaki yan,

**[04:18:46.960] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003699)  
yung commitment po in partnership with the Department of Education

**[04:18:51.720] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003700)  
to build 125 school buildings all over the Philippines

**[04:18:57.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003701)  
which will have an equivalent of 1,500 new classrooms po.

**[04:19:04.359] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003702)  
Maraming salamat.

**[04:19:05.960] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003703)  
Malaking tulong po sa mga distrito yan.

**[04:19:10.199] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003704)  
Yung classrooms.

**[04:19:11.080] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003705)  
Bukod po diyan, kasama po diyan yung mga ambulansya.

**[04:19:14.040] Rep. Antonino B. Roman III** (asr-003706)  
At ano po yung ibang klase ng tulong ang ibinababa po ng PAGCOR?

**[04:19:19.040] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003707)  
Yung tulong po namin ay meron pong PTV,

**[04:19:24.159] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003708)  
Patient Transport Vehicles.

**[04:19:25.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-003709)

**[04:33:11.279] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004103)  
Tingnan natin.

**[04:33:11.879] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004104)

**[04:33:15.480] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004106)

**[04:33:18.000] Rep. Atty. Miro S. Quimbo** (asr-004108)

**[04:33:21.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004109)  
Opo.

**[04:33:22.119] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004110)  
Para matinan po natin yung inyong kahilingan.

**[04:33:24.719] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004111)

**[04:33:29.240] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004112)  
Dati pong PAGCOR ang pondo dito.

**[04:33:31.160] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004113)  
PAGCOR talaga. PAGCOR ang gumawa.

**[04:33:33.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004115)  
PAGCOR building po yun.

**[04:33:34.520] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004116)  
Opo, tingnan natin.

**[04:33:35.320] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004117)  
At iba pa po ang chairman noon.

**[04:33:37.799] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004118)

**[04:33:41.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004119)

**[04:33:46.480] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004121)

**[04:33:49.320] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004122)

**[04:33:50.000] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004123)

**[04:34:17.320] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-004135)  
Hello, Madam Chair.

**[04:34:24.680] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-004136)  
This representation would like to thank and congratulate the PAGCOR for the continuous good work and service to our society.

**[04:34:42.080] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-004138)  
I have a question about the hospitals.

**[04:34:45.480] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-004139)  
Are you also funding hospitals?

**[04:34:49.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004140)  
Your Honor, we are not.

**[04:34:55.600] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004141)  
PAGCOR, as it is, is not funding the construction of hospitals.

**[04:35:00.359] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004142)  
But rather, our foundations are the ones helping out.

**[04:35:07.561] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004143)  
And recently, most recently...

**[04:35:09.959] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004144)  
Just in the last week of July, we inaugurated a multi-specialty hospital in Clark.

**[04:35:18.719] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004145)  
And that was funded by Bloomberry Foundation, which is the foundation arm of Solair.

**[04:35:28.920] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004146)  
So they did budget and they spent more than a billion pesos.

**[04:35:35.480] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004147)  
And PAGCOR helped in purchasing equipment.

**[04:35:39.004] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004148)  
And now, we have the equipment that is going to be used for the said hospital.

**[04:35:44.320] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004149)  
So, that is the situation.

**[04:35:47.279] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004150)  
Case-to-case basis, it is the foundations of the different licenses that normally fund these big-ticket items.

**[04:36:00.719] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004151)  
Because, Your Honor, when it comes to big-tickets, we will be spreading ourselves very thinly.

**[04:36:07.160] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004152)  
So, we solicit the help.

**[04:36:08.760] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004153)  
The help of the foundations of the different hospitals.

**[04:36:13.680] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004154)  
Another case is the Conrado Estrella Hospital in Pangasinan.

**[04:36:20.240] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004155)  
I know, Your Honor, that you are there.

**[04:36:23.319] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004156)  
It is just a small hospital.

**[04:36:25.799] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004157)  
But we were able to, I think, give 100 million pesos during the time of my predecessor.

**[04:36:34.160] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004158)  
And then, there was only equipment needed.

**[04:36:37.139] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004159)  
That is where I came in and we helped in the Conrado Estrella District Hospital.

**[04:36:46.439] PAGCOR Chairman and CEO Alejandro H. Tengco** (asr-004160)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[04:36:51.480] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-004161)

**[04:36:59.160] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-004162)  
I represent the 2nd District of Pangasinan.

**[04:37:03.840] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-004163)  
So, this representation...

**[04:37:10.719] Rep. Mark O. Cojuangco** (asr-004171)

you were talking about.
